              '\n'
              '  @override\n'

              '    _emailController.dispose();\n'
              '    _passwordController.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _initialize() async {\n'
              '    final hasAuthRepository = widget.authRepository != null;\n'
              '    final hasLearningRepository = widget.learningRepository != null;\n'
              '    if (hasAuthRepository != hasLearningRepository) {\n'
              '      throw ArgumentError(\n'
              '        \'authRepository and learningRepository must be supplied together.\',\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (hasAuthRepository && hasLearningRepository) {\n'
              '      _authRepository = widget.authRepository;\n'
              '      _learningRepository = widget.learningRepository;\n'
              '      _progressiveCaseRepository = widget.progressiveCaseRepository;\n'
              '      _evidenceAttachmentRepository = widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository;\n'
              '      _organizationManagementRepository =\n'
              '          widget.organizationManagementRepository;\n'
              '      _invitationAcceptanceRepository = widget.invitationAcceptanceRepository;\n'
              '      _organizationMemberProvisioningRepository =\n'
              '          widget.organizationMemberProvisioningRepository;\n'
              '      _organizationMemberLifecycleRepository =\n'
              '          widget.organizationMemberLifecycleRepository;\n'
              '      _teacherLearningRecordRepository = widget.teacherLearningRecordRepository;\n'
              '      _studentLearningRecordRepository = widget.studentLearningRecordRepository;\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      widget.config.cloudConfig.validate(\n'
              '        requireConfigured: widget.config.environment.isProduction,\n'
              '        requireHttps: widget.config.environment.isProduction,\n'
              '        requireAllowedHost: widget.config.environment.isProduction,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (!widget.config.cloudConfig.isConfigured) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      await CloudClient.initialize(\n'
              '        widget.config.cloudConfig,\n'
              '        requireSecureEndpoint: widget.config.environment.isProduction,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      _authRepository = SupabaseAuthRepository(CloudClient.client);\n'
              '      _learningRepository = SupabaseLearningRepository(CloudClient.client);\n'
              '      _progressiveCaseRepository = SupabaseProgressiveCaseRepository(\n'
              '        CloudClient.client,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      _evidenceAttachmentRepository = SupabaseEvidenceAttachmentRepository(\n'
              '        CloudClient.client,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      _organizationManagementRepository =\n'
              '          SupabaseOrganizationManagementRepository(CloudClient.client);\n'
              '      _invitationAcceptanceRepository =\n'
              '          SupabaseOrganizationInvitationAcceptanceRepository(\n'
              '            CloudClient.client,\n'
              '          );\n'
              '      _organizationMemberProvisioningRepository =\n'
              '          SupabaseOrganizationMemberProvisioningRepository(CloudClient.client);\n'
              '      _organizationMemberLifecycleRepository =\n'
              '          SupabaseOrganizationMemberLifecycleRepository(CloudClient.client);\n'
              '      _teacherLearningRecordRepository =\n'
              '          SupabaseTeacherLearningRecordRepository(CloudClient.client);\n'
              '      _studentLearningRecordRepository =\n'
              '          SupabaseStudentLearningRecordRepository(CloudClient.client);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    _activeUserId = _authRepository!.currentUser?.id;\n'
              '    _signedIn = _activeUserId != null;\n'
              '    if (_signedIn && _organizationMemberLifecycleRepository != null) {\n'
              '      _checkingMembershipState = true;\n'
              '      unawaited(_loadMembershipState());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _authSubscription = _authRepository!.authStateChanges.listen((state) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final nextUserId = state.session?.user.id;\n'
              '      final userChanged = _activeUserId != nextUserId;\n'
              '      if (_onboardingTransition && nextUserId == null) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _activeUserId = nextUserId;\n'
              '        _signedIn = nextUserId != null;\n'
              '        if (!_signedIn || userChanged) {\n'
              '          _errorMessage = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (!_signedIn) {\n'
              '          _membershipState = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      if (nextUserId != null &&\n'
              '          userChanged &&\n'
              '          _organizationMemberLifecycleRepository != null) {\n'
              '        unawaited(_loadMembershipState());\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _loadMembershipState() async {\n'
              '    final repository = _organizationMemberLifecycleRepository;\n'
              '    if (repository == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (mounted) {\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _checkingMembershipState = true;\n'
              '        _membershipState = null;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final state = await repository.loadCurrentMembershipState();\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _membershipState = state);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(\n'
              '          () => _errorMessage = _describeAuthError(error, action: \'读取账号状态\'),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _checkingMembershipState = false);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _retryInitialization() {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _initialization = _initialize();\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _signIn() async {\n'
              '    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate() || _authRepository == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _busy = true;\n'
              '      _errorMessage = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _authRepository!.signIn(\n'
              '        email: _emailController.text.trim(),\n'
              '        password: _passwordController.text,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      final nextUserId = _authRepository!.currentUser?.id;\n'
              '      if (nextUserId == null) {\n'
              '        throw const AuthException(\'Authentication did not return a user.\');\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _activeUserId = nextUserId;\n'
              '          _signedIn = true;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        if (_organizationMemberLifecycleRepository != null) {\n'
              '          unawaited(_loadMembershipState());\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      _passwordController.clear();\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _errorMessage = _describeAuthError(error, action: \'登录\');\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _busy = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _signOut() async {\n'
              '    final authRepository = _authRepository;\n'
              '    if (authRepository == null || _busy) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _busy = true;\n'
              '      _errorMessage = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await authRepository.signOut();\n'
              '      if (authRepository.currentUser != null) {\n'
              '        await authRepository.signOut(global: false);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        final nextUserId = authRepository.currentUser?.id;\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _activeUserId = nextUserId;\n'
              '          _signedIn = nextUserId != null;\n'
              '          if (!_signedIn) {\n'
              '            _emailController.clear();\n'
              '            _passwordController.clear();\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        final nextUserId = authRepository.currentUser?.id;\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _activeUserId = nextUserId;\n'
              '          _signedIn = nextUserId != null;\n'
              '          _errorMessage = _describeAuthError(error, action: \'退出登录\');\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _busy = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _describeAuthError(Object error, {required String action}) {\n'
              '    if (error is AuthException) {\n'
              '      final detail = error.message.trim();\n'
              '      if (detail.toLowerCase() == \'invalid login credentials\') {\n'
              '        return \'$action失败：账号或密码不正确。\';\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (detail.isEmpty) {\n'
              '        return \'$action失败，请检查网络后重试。\';\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return \'$action失败：$detail\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return \'$action失败，请检查网络后重试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return FutureBuilder<void>(\n'
              '      future: _initialization,\n'
              '      builder: (context, snapshot) {\n'
              '        if (snapshot.connectionState != ConnectionState.done) {\n'

              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'

              '            child: _WorkspaceConfigBody(),\n'

              '        }\n'

              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (_organizationMemberLifecycleRepository != null &&\n'
              '            (_checkingMembershipState || _membershipState == null)) {\n'
              '          return _WorkspaceStatusScaffold(\n'
              '            title: \'教师工作台\',\n'
              '            child: _checkingMembershipState\n'
              '                ? const _WorkspaceLoadingBody(message: \'正在核验账号状态…\')\n'
              '                : _WorkspaceErrorBody(\n'
              '                    title: \'账号状态读取失败\',\n'
              '                    message: _errorMessage ?? \'请重试后继续。\',\n'
              '                    onRetry: () => unawaited(_loadMembershipState()),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        final membershipState = _membershipState;\n'
              '        if (membershipState?.isOnboarding == true) {\n'
              '          final email =\n'
              '              _authRepository!.currentUser?.email ??\n'
              '              _emailController.text.trim();\n'
              '          return MemberOnboardingPage(\n'
              '            key: ValueKey(\'onboarding-$_activeUserId\'),\n'
              '            authRepository: _authRepository!,\n'
              '            lifecycleRepository: _organizationMemberLifecycleRepository!,\n'
              '            email: email,\n'
              '            displayName: membershipState?.displayName,\n'
              '            expiresAt: membershipState?.onboardingExpiresAt,\n'
              '            onTransitionChanged: _setOnboardingTransition,\n'
              '            onCompleted: _finishOnboarding,\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (membershipState?.isDisabled == true) {\n'
              '          return _WorkspaceStatusScaffold(\n'
              '            title: \'账号已停用\',\n'
              '            child: _WorkspaceErrorBody(\n'
              '              title: \'暂时无法进入工作台\',\n'
              '              message: \'当前账号已被机构负责人停用，请联系负责人处理。\',\n'
              '              onRetry: () => unawaited(_signOut()),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        return TeacherWorkspacePage(\n'
              '          key: ValueKey(_activeUserId),\n'
              '          repository: _learningRepository!,\n'
              '          progressiveCaseRepository: _progressiveCaseRepository,\n'
              '          evidenceAttachmentRepository: _evidenceAttachmentRepository,\n'
              '          managementRepository: _organizationManagementRepository,\n'
              '          memberProvisioningRepository:\n'
              '              _organizationMemberProvisioningRepository,\n'
              '          invitationAcceptanceRepository: _invitationAcceptanceRepository,\n'
              '          teacherLearningRecordRepository: _teacherLearningRecordRepository,\n'
              '          studentLearningRecordRepository: _studentLearningRecordRepository,\n'
              '          updateService: _updateService,\n'
              '          updateInstaller: _updateInstaller,\n'
              '          onSignOut: _busy ? null : _signOut,\n'
              '          caseReopenDraftStore: _caseReopenDraftStore,\n'
              '          sessionUserId: _activeUserId,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _setOnboardingTransition(bool value) {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _onboardingTransition = value;\n'
              '      if (!value && _authRepository?.currentUser == null) {\n'
              '        _activeUserId = null;\n'
              '        _signedIn = false;\n'
              '        _membershipState = null;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _finishOnboarding() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _onboardingTransition = false;\n'
              '      _activeUserId = null;\n'
              '      _signedIn = false;\n'
              '      _membershipState = null;\n'
              '      _passwordController.clear();\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'

              '  bool _showAllStudentCases = false;\n'
              '  bool _showAllCaseTimeline = false;\n'
              '  bool _showCaseCategoryDetails = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _workspaceFuture = _loadWorkspace();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<TeacherWorkspace> _loadWorkspace() async {\n'
              '    final requestId = ++_loadSequence;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final workspace = await widget.repository.loadWorkspace();\n'
              '      if (mounted && requestId == _loadSequence) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _lastWorkspace = workspace;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return workspace;\n'
              '    } catch (error, stackTrace) {\n'
              '      _workspaceLogger.error(\n'
              '        \'Teacher workspace load failed.\',\n'
              '        error: error,\n'
              '        stackTrace: stackTrace,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      rethrow;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _checkForUpdates() async {\n'
              '    final service = widget.updateService;\n'
              '    final installer = widget.updateInstaller;\n'
              '    if (service == null || installer == null || _checkingForUpdates) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    setState(() => _checkingForUpdates = true);\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final result = await service.checkForUpdate();\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final shouldInstall = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        builder: (_) => UpdateDialog(result: result),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (shouldInstall != true || !mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      final downloaded = await service.download(result);\n'
              '      final installResult = await installer.install(downloaded);\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (installResult.shouldExit) {\n'
              '        await SystemNavigator.pop();\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '          .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text(\'已打开系统安装界面，请按提示完成更新。\')));\n'
              '    } on UpdateException catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(error.userMessage)));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } on UpdateInstallException catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(error.userMessage)));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } on Object {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text(\'更新失败，请稍后重试。\')));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _checkingForUpdates = false);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _studentSearchController.dispose();\n'
              '    _inviteCodeController.dispose();\n'
              '    _displayNameController.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _selectDestination(int index) {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _selectedIndex = index;\n'
              '      _selectedStudent = null;\n'
              '      _selectedCase = null;\n'
              '      _showAllStudentCases = false;\n'
              '      _showAllCaseTimeline = false;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _openStudent(WorkspaceStudent student) {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _selectedStudent = student;\n'
              '      _selectedCase = null;\n'
              '      _showAllStudentCases = false;\n'
              '      _showAllCaseTimeline = false;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _openCase(WorkspaceStudent student, WorkspaceCase learningCase) {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _selectedStudent = student;\n'
              '      _selectedCase = learningCase;\n'
              '      _showAllCaseTimeline = false;\n'
              '      _showCaseCategoryDetails = false;\n'
              '      _showAllCaseTimeline = false;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _goBack() {\n'
              '    if (_selectedCase != null) {\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _selectedCase = null;\n'
              '        _showAllCaseTimeline = false;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_selectedStudent != null) {\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _selectedStudent = null;\n'
              '        _showAllStudentCases = false;\n'
              '        _showAllCaseTimeline = false;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<bool> _reload({\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent? preserveStudent,\n'
              '    String? preserveCaseId,\n'
              '  }) async {\n'
              '    final nextFuture = _loadWorkspace();\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _workspaceFuture = nextFuture;\n'
              '      _isRefreshing = _lastWorkspace != null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final workspace = await nextFuture;\n'
              '      if (!mounted || preserveStudent == null) {\n'
              '        return true;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      WorkspaceStudent? matchingStudent;\n'
              '      for (final student in workspace.students) {\n'
              '        if (student.profileId == preserveStudent.profileId) {\n'
              '          matchingStudent = student;\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (matchingStudent != null) {\n'
              '        WorkspaceCase? matchingCase;\n'
              '        if (preserveCaseId != null) {\n'
              '          for (final learningCase in matchingStudent.cases) {\n'
              '            if (learningCase.id == preserveCaseId) {\n'
              '              matchingCase = learningCase;\n'
              '              break;\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _selectedStudent = matchingStudent;\n'
              '          if (preserveCaseId != null) {\n'
              '            _selectedCase = matchingCase;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return true;\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          // Never leave a write result hidden behind stale workspace data.\n'
              '          _lastWorkspace = null;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text(\'刷新失败，请点击重试确认最新数据。\')));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _isRefreshing = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _closeRetryKeyFor(WorkspaceCase learningCase) {\n'
              '    return \'close:${learningCase.id}:${learningCase.version}\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _operationIdForRetry(String retryKey) {\n'
              '    return _retryOperationIds.putIfAbsent(retryKey, createOperationId);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _clearRetry(String retryKey) {\n'
              '    _retryOperationIds.remove(retryKey);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _rescheduleRetryKeyFor(\n'
              '    WorkspaceActionWithContext item,\n'
              '    DateTime picked,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    final dateKey =\n'
              '        \'${picked.year.toString().padLeft(4, \'0\')}-\'\n'
              '        \'${picked.month.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\')}-\'\n'
              '        \'${picked.day.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\')}\';\n'
              '    return \'reschedule:${item.learningCase.id}:${item.action.id}:\'\n'
              '        \'${item.learningCase.version}:${item.action.version}:$dateKey\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _acceptInvitation() async {\n'
              '    if (_invitationBusy ||\n'
              '        !(_invitationFormKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false)) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final repository = widget.invitationAcceptanceRepository;\n'
              '    if (repository == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _invitationBusy = true;\n'
              '      _invitationErrorMessage = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await repository.acceptInvitation(\n'
              '        inviteCode: _inviteCodeController.text.trim(),\n'
              '        displayName: _displayNameController.text.trim(),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      _inviteCodeController.clear();\n'
              '      _displayNameController.clear();\n'
              '      await _reload();\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text(\'已加入机构，可以开始使用。\')));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _invitationErrorMessage = _describeInvitationError(error);\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _invitationBusy = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _describeInvitationError(Object error) {\n'
              '    final knownMessage = organizationInvitationErrorMessage(error);\n'
              '    if (knownMessage != null) {\n'
              '      return \'接受邀请失败：$knownMessage\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return \'接受邀请失败，请检查代码、登录邮箱和网络后重试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _showQuickCapture({WorkspaceStudent? student}) async {\n'
              '    final workspace = await _workspaceFuture;\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final sizeClass = ResponsiveBreakpoints.classify(\n'
              '      MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final result = sizeClass == WindowSizeClass.compact\n'
              '        ? await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(\n'
              '            context: context,\n'
              '            isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '            isDismissible: false,\n'
              '            enableDrag: false,\n'
              '            backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,\n'
              '            shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(\n'
              '              borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(28)),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,\n'
              '            builder: (context) => _WorkspaceQuickCaptureForm(\n'
              '              students: workspace.students,\n'
              '              caseTypes: workspace.caseTypes,\n'
              '              initialStudent: student,\n'
              '              repository: widget.repository,\n'
              '              organizationId: workspace.organizationId,\n'
              '              evidenceAttachmentRepository: widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        : await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '            context: context,\n'
              '            barrierDismissible: false,\n'
              '            builder: (context) => Dialog(\n'
              '              child: _WorkspaceQuickCaptureForm(\n'
              '                students: workspace.students,\n'
              '                caseTypes: workspace.caseTypes,\n'
              '                initialStudent: student,\n'
              '                repository: widget.repository,\n'
              '                organizationId: workspace.organizationId,\n'
              '                evidenceAttachmentRepository:\n'
              '                    widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (!mounted || result != true) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await _reload(preserveStudent: student);\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '        .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text(\'已记录到学生成长记录。\')));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _showCaseTypeManager() async {\n'
              '    final workspace = await _workspaceFuture;\n'
              '    final organizationId = workspace.organizationId;\n'
              '    if (!mounted || !workspace.canManageCaseTypes || organizationId == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final sizeClass = ResponsiveBreakpoints.classify(\n'
              '      MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final manager = _WorkspaceCaseTypeManager(\n'
              '      organizationId: organizationId,\n'
              '      caseTypes: workspace.caseTypes,\n'
              '      repository: widget.repository,\n'
              '      onChanged: () => _reload(),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (sizeClass == WindowSizeClass.compact) {\n'
              '      await showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '        isDismissible: false,\n'
              '        enableDrag: false,\n'
              '        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,\n'
              '        shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(\n'
              '          borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(28)),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,\n'
              '        builder: (_) => manager,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      await showDialog<void>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        barrierDismissible: false,\n'
              '        builder: (_) => Dialog(child: manager),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _recordProgress(\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent student,\n'
              '    WorkspaceCase learningCase, {\n'
              '    WorkspaceAction? currentAction,\n'
              '    bool completeCurrentActionInitially = false,\n'
              '    bool preserveSelection = false,\n'
              '    bool preserveStudentOnly = false,\n'
              '  }) async {\n'
              '    final repository = widget.progressiveCaseRepository;\n'
              '    if (repository == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final workspace = await _workspaceFuture;\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final result = await showCaseProgressForm(\n'
              '      context,\n'
              '      repository: repository,\n'
              '      learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '      currentAction: currentAction,\n'
              '      completeCurrentActionInitially: completeCurrentActionInitially,\n'
              '      businessDate: workspace.businessDate,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || result == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final reloaded = preserveSelection\n'
              '        ? await _reload(\n'
              '            preserveStudent: student,\n'
              '            preserveCaseId: learningCase.id,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        : preserveStudentOnly\n'
              '        ? await _reload(preserveStudent: student)\n'
              '        : await _reload();\n'
              '    if (!mounted || !reloaded) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final message = switch (result.nextStep) {\n'
              '      \'remind\' => \'已记录进展，并设置提醒。\',\n'
              '      \'close\' => \'已记录进展，并结束跟进。\',\n'
              '      _ => \'已记录进展。\',\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _endProgressiveFollowUp(\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent student,\n'
              '    WorkspaceCase learningCase, {\n'
              '    bool preserveSelection = false,\n'
              '  }) async {\n'
              '    final repository = widget.progressiveCaseRepository;\n'
              '    if (repository == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final result = await showEndCaseFollowUpForm(\n'
              '      context,\n'
              '      repository: repository,\n'
              '      learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || result == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final reloaded = preserveSelection\n'
              '        ? await _reload(\n'
              '            preserveStudent: student,\n'
              '            preserveCaseId: learningCase.id,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        : await _reload();\n'
              '    if (!mounted || !reloaded) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '        .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text(\'已结束跟进，历史记录完整保留。\')));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<CaseCommandReceipt?> _showCaseForm({\n'
              '    required _CaseCommandMode mode,\n'
              '    required WorkspaceCase learningCase,\n'
              '    DateTime? businessDate,\n'
              '  }) {\n'
              '    final sizeClass = ResponsiveBreakpoints.classify(\n'
              '      MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final form = _WorkspaceCaseCommandForm(\n'
              '      mode: mode,\n'
              '      learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '      repository: widget.repository,\n'
              '      businessDate: businessDate,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return sizeClass == WindowSizeClass.compact\n'
              '        ? showModalBottomSheet<CaseCommandReceipt>(\n'
              '            context: context,\n'
              '            isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '            isDismissible: false,\n'
              '            enableDrag: false,\n'
              '            backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,\n'
              '            shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(\n'
              '              borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(28)),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,\n'
              '            builder: (_) => form,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        : showDialog<CaseCommandReceipt>(\n'
              '            context: context,\n'
              '            barrierDismissible: false,\n'
              '            builder: (_) => Dialog(child: form),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<CaseCommandReceipt?> _showReopenForm({\n'
              '    required WorkspaceCase learningCase,\n'
              '    DateTime? businessDate,\n'
              '    String? draftScopeKey,\n'
              '  }) {\n'
              '    final sizeClass = ResponsiveBreakpoints.classify(\n'
              '      MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final form = _WorkspaceReopenCaseForm(\n'
              '      learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '      repository: widget.repository,\n'
              '      businessDate: businessDate,\n'
              '      draftStore:\n'
              '          widget.caseReopenDraftStore ?? const NoopCaseReopenDraftStore(),\n'
              '      draftScopeKey: draftScopeKey,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return sizeClass == WindowSizeClass.compact\n'
              '        ? showModalBottomSheet<CaseCommandReceipt>(\n'
              '            context: context,\n'
              '            isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '            isDismissible: false,\n'
              '            enableDrag: false,\n'
              '            backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,\n'
              '            shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(\n'
              '              borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(28)),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,\n'
              '            builder: (_) => form,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        : showDialog<CaseCommandReceipt>(\n'
              '            context: context,\n'
              '            barrierDismissible: false,\n'
              '            builder: (_) => Dialog(child: form),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _showCaseCommand(\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent student,\n'
              '    WorkspaceCase learningCase,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    final mode = _caseCommandMode(learningCase);\n'
              '    if (mode == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final workspace = await _workspaceFuture;\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final result = await _showCaseForm(\n'
              '      mode: mode,\n'
              '      learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '      businessDate: workspace.businessDate,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || result == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await _reload(preserveStudent: student, preserveCaseId: learningCase.id);\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '      SnackBar(\n'
              '        content: Text(\'已保存，当前状态：${_caseStatusLabelFromWire(result.status)}。\'),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _showStabilizeCase(\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent student,\n'
              '    WorkspaceCase learningCase,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    final workspace = await _workspaceFuture;\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final result = await _showCaseForm(\n'
              '      mode: _CaseCommandMode.stabilize,\n'
              '      learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '      businessDate: workspace.businessDate,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || result == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await _reload(preserveStudent: student, preserveCaseId: learningCase.id);\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '      SnackBar(\n'
              '        content: Text(\'已保存，当前状态：${_caseStatusLabelFromWire(result.status)}。\'),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _showReopenCase(\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent student,\n'
              '    WorkspaceCase learningCase,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    final workspace = await _workspaceFuture;\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final result = await _showReopenForm(\n'
              '      learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '      businessDate: workspace.businessDate,\n'
              '      draftScopeKey: _caseReopenDraftScopeKey(\n'
              '        sessionUserId: widget.sessionUserId,\n'
              '        organizationId: workspace.organizationId,\n'
              '        caseId: learningCase.id,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || result == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await _reload(preserveStudent: student, preserveCaseId: learningCase.id);\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '      SnackBar(\n'
              '        content: Text(\'已保存，当前状态：${_caseStatusLabelFromWire(result.status)}。\'),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _closeCase(\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent student,\n'
              '    WorkspaceCase learningCase,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    final shouldClose = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '        title: const Text(\'结束跟进？\'),\n'
              '        content: const Text(\'结束后不会再列入当前待跟进事项，但学生的历史记录会完整保留。\'),\n'
              '        actions: [\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'取消\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          FilledButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'结束跟进\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || shouldClose != true) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final retryKey = _closeRetryKeyFor(learningCase);\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final receipt = await widget.repository.closeCase(\n'
              '        CloseCaseCommand(\n'
              '          operationId: _operationIdForRetry(retryKey),\n'
              '          caseId: learningCase.id,\n'
              '          expectedCaseVersion: learningCase.version,\n'
              '          closedAt: null,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      await _reload(preserveStudent: student, preserveCaseId: learningCase.id);\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      _clearRetry(retryKey);\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '        SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\n'
              '            \'已保存，当前状态：${_caseStatusLabelFromWire(receipt.status)}。\',\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(\n'
              '        context,\n'
              '      ).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(_describeCaseCommandError(error))));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _rescheduleAction(\n'
              '    TeacherWorkspace workspace,\n'
              '    WorkspaceActionWithContext item,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    if (_reschedulingActionId != null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final businessNow = workspace.businessDate ?? DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final today = DateTime(\n'
              '      businessNow.year,\n'
              '      businessNow.month,\n'
              '      businessNow.day,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final lastDate = DateTime(today.year + 2, today.month, today.day);\n'
              '    final existing =\n'
              '        item.action.businessDueDate ?? item.action.dueAt?.toLocal();\n'
              '    final existingDate = existing == null\n'
              '        ? null\n'
              '        : DateTime(existing.year, existing.month, existing.day);\n'
              '    final initialDate = existingDate == null || existingDate.isBefore(today)\n'
              '        ? today\n'
              '        : existingDate.isAfter(lastDate)\n'
              '        ? lastDate\n'
              '        : existingDate;\n'
              '    final picked = await showDatePicker(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      firstDate: today,\n'
              '      lastDate: lastDate,\n'
              '      initialDate: initialDate,\n'
              '      helpText: item.action.dueAt == null ? \'安排行动日期\' : \'改期行动\',\n'
              '      confirmText: \'保存\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || picked == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final retryKey = _rescheduleRetryKeyFor(item, picked);\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _reschedulingActionId = item.action.id;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await widget.repository.rescheduleCaseAction(\n'
              '        RescheduleCaseActionCommand(\n'
              '          operationId: _operationIdForRetry(retryKey),\n'
              '          actionId: item.action.id,\n'
              '          caseId: item.learningCase.id,\n'
              '          expectedCaseVersion: item.learningCase.version,\n'
              '          expectedActionVersion: item.action.version,\n'
              '          dueOn: DateTime(picked.year, picked.month, picked.day),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      await _reload();\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      _clearRetry(retryKey);\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '        SnackBar(content: Text(\'行动已安排在${_formatDateOnly(picked)}。\')),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(\n'
              '        context,\n'
              '      ).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(_describeCaseCommandError(error))));\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _reschedulingActionId = null;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _completeAction(\n'
              '    TeacherWorkspace workspace,\n'
              '    WorkspaceActionWithContext item,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    if (_completingActionId != null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() => _completingActionId = item.action.id);\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final result = await _showCompleteActionForm(\n'
              '        item: item,\n'
              '        businessDate: workspace.businessDate,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (!mounted || result == null) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final reloaded = await _reload();\n'
              '      if (!mounted || !reloaded) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '        SnackBar(content: Text(result ? \'已记录进展并完成提醒。\' : \'已完成提醒。\')),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _completingActionId = null);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<bool?> _showCompleteActionForm({\n'
              '    required WorkspaceActionWithContext item,\n'
              '    required DateTime? businessDate,\n'
              '  }) {\n'
              '    final sizeClass = ResponsiveBreakpoints.classify(\n'
              '      MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final form = _WorkspaceCompleteActionForm(\n'
              '      action: item.action,\n'
              '      learningCase: item.learningCase,\n'
              '      repository: widget.repository,\n'
              '      progressiveRepository: widget.progressiveCaseRepository,\n'
              '      businessDate: businessDate,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return sizeClass == WindowSizeClass.compact\n'
              '        ? showModalBottomSheet<bool>(\n'
              '            context: context,\n'
              '            isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '            isDismissible: false,\n'
              '            enableDrag: false,\n'
              '            backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,\n'
              '            shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(\n'
              '              borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(28)),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,\n'
              '            builder: (_) => form,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        : showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '            context: context,\n'
              '            barrierDismissible: false,\n'
              '            builder: (_) => Dialog(child: form),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final workspace = _lastWorkspace;\n'
              '    if (workspace != null) {\n'
              '      return Stack(\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          _buildLoadedWorkspace(workspace),\n'
              '          if (_isRefreshing)\n'
              '            const Positioned(\n'
              '              top: 0,\n'
              '              left: 0,\n'
              '              right: 0,\n'
              '              child: LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 2),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    return FutureBuilder<TeacherWorkspace>(\n'
              '      future: _workspaceFuture,\n'
              '      builder: (context, snapshot) {\n'
              '        if (snapshot.connectionState != ConnectionState.done) {\n'
              '          return const _WorkspaceStatusScaffold(\n'
              '            title: \'教师工作台\',\n'
              '            child: _WorkspaceLoadingBody(message: \'正在加载学生和今日事项…\'),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (snapshot.hasError || !snapshot.hasData) {\n'
              '          return _WorkspaceStatusScaffold(\n'
              '            title: \'教师工作台\',\n'
              '            child: _WorkspaceErrorBody(\n'
              '              title: \'暂时无法加载工作台\',\n'
              '              message: _describeWorkspaceLoadError(snapshot.error),\n'
              '              onRetry: () => _reload(),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        return _buildLoadedWorkspace(snapshot.data!);\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildLoadedWorkspace(TeacherWorkspace workspace) {\n'
              '    if (!workspace.hasTeachingAccess && !workspace.canManageOrganization) {\n'
              '      if (workspace.canManageCaseTypes && workspace.organizationId != null) {\n'
              '        return _WorkspaceStatusScaffold(\n'
              '          title: \'机构问题类型\',\n'
              '          child: _WorkspaceCaseTypeManager(\n'
              '            organizationId: workspace.organizationId!,\n'
              '            caseTypes: workspace.caseTypes,\n'
              '            repository: widget.repository,\n'
              '            onChanged: () => _reload(),\n'
              '            showCloseButton: false,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return _WorkspaceStatusScaffold(\n'
              '        title: \'教师工作台\',\n'
              '        child: _WorkspaceNoAccessBody(\n'
              '          invitationAcceptanceCard: _buildInvitationAcceptanceCard(),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    return PopScope<void>(\n'
              '      canPop: _selectedStudent == null && _selectedCase == null,\n'
              '      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {\n'
              '        if (!didPop) {\n'
              '          _goBack();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      child: _WorkspaceShell(\n'
              '        selectedIndex: _selectedIndex,\n'
              '        onDestinationSelected: _selectDestination,\n'
              '        hasTeachingAccess: workspace.hasTeachingAccess,\n'
              '        showManagement: workspace.canManageOrganization,\n'
              '        onSignOut: widget.onSignOut,\n'
              '        onCheckForUpdates:\n'
              '            widget.updateService == null || widget.updateInstaller == null\n'
              '            ? null\n'
              '            : () => unawaited(_checkForUpdates()),\n'
              '        checkingForUpdates: _checkingForUpdates,\n'
              '        child: ResponsiveLayout(\n'
              '          builder: (context, sizeClass) => _WorkspaceFrame(\n'
              '            sizeClass: sizeClass,\n'
              '            child: _buildCurrentPage(workspace, sizeClass),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildCurrentPage(\n'
              '    TeacherWorkspace workspace,\n'
              '    WindowSizeClass sizeClass,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    if (_selectedCase != null && _selectedStudent != null) {\n'
              '      return _buildCaseDetail(workspace, _selectedStudent!, _selectedCase!);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_selectedStudent != null) {\n'
              '      return _buildStudentDetail(_selectedStudent!, sizeClass);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final managementIndex = workspace.hasTeachingAccess ? 2 : 0;\n'
              '    if (workspace.canManageOrganization && _selectedIndex == managementIndex) {\n'
              '      return _buildManagement(workspace);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!workspace.hasTeachingAccess) {\n'
              '      return _WorkspaceNoAccessBody(\n'
              '        invitationAcceptanceCard: _buildInvitationAcceptanceCard(),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return _selectedIndex == 0\n'
              '        ? _buildToday(workspace)\n'
              '        : _buildStudents(workspace);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildManagement(TeacherWorkspace workspace) {\n'
              '    final organizationId = workspace.organizationId;\n'
              '    final managementRepository = widget.managementRepository;\n'
              '    if (organizationId == null || managementRepository == null) {\n'
              '      return const _WorkspaceManagementUnavailable();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return OrganizationManagementPage(\n'
              '      repository: managementRepository,\n'
              '      provisioningRepository: widget.memberProvisioningRepository,\n'
              '      teacherLearningRecordRepository: widget.teacherLearningRecordRepository,\n'
              '      studentLearningRecordRepository: widget.studentLearningRecordRepository,\n'
              '      organizationId: organizationId,\n'
              '      organizationName: workspace.organizationName,\n'
              '      roles: workspace.roles,\n'
              '      canManageCaseTypes: workspace.canManageCaseTypes,\n'
              '      onOpenCaseTypes: workspace.canManageCaseTypes\n'
              '          ? () => unawaited(_showCaseTypeManager())\n'
              '          : null,\n'
              '      onChanged: () => unawaited(_reload()),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget? _buildInvitationAcceptanceCard() {\n'
              '    if (widget.invitationAcceptanceRepository == null) {\n'
              '      return null;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return OrganizationInvitationAcceptanceCard(\n'
              '      formKey: _invitationFormKey,\n'
              '      inviteCodeController: _inviteCodeController,\n'
              '      displayNameController: _displayNameController,\n'
              '      busy: _invitationBusy,\n'
              '      initiallyExpanded: true,\n'
              '      errorMessage: _invitationErrorMessage,\n'
              '      onAccept: _acceptInvitation,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildToday(TeacherWorkspace workspace) {\n'
              '    final actions = <WorkspaceActionWithContext>[];\n'
              '    for (final student in workspace.students) {\n'
              '      for (final learningCase in student.cases) {\n'
              '        if (learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.newCase &&\n'
              '            learningCase.primaryAction == null) {\n'
              '          // A newly recorded fact is not a task. It remains on the student\'s\n'
              '          // growth record until the teacher explicitly creates a reminder.\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.pendingVerification &&\n'
              '            learningCase.primaryAction == null) {\n'
              '          // A completed check is a student fact, not a second confirmation task.\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        final action = learningCase.primaryAction;\n'
              '        if (action == null ||\n'
              '            learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.closed) {\n'
              '          continue;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        actions.add(\n'
              '          WorkspaceActionWithContext(\n'
              '            student: student,\n'
              '            learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '            action: action,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final overdue = _actionsInBucket(actions, WorkspaceActionBucket.overdue);\n'
              '    final today = _actionsInBucket(actions, WorkspaceActionBucket.today);\n'
              '    final future = _actionsInBucket(actions, WorkspaceActionBucket.future);\n'
              '    final undated = _actionsInBucket(actions, WorkspaceActionBucket.undated);\n'
              '    final recentStudents = _dedupeStudentsById(\n'
              '      _studentsByRecentActivity(workspace.students),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final hasScheduledWork = overdue.isNotEmpty || today.isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    final hasImmediateWork = hasScheduledWork || undated.isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    final hasBlockBeforeFuture = hasScheduledWork || !hasImmediateWork;\n'
              '    final hasBlockBeforeUndated = hasBlockBeforeFuture || future.isNotEmpty;\n'
              '\n'
              '    return Column(\n'
              '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        _WorkspacePageHeader(\n'
              '          title: \'今日\',\n'
              '          subtitle: \'先处理今天要做的事，再回看需要判断的学生。\',\n'
              '          actions: [\n'
              '            FilledButton.icon(\n'
              '              onPressed: () => _showQuickCapture(),\n'
              '              icon: Icon(Icons.edit_note_outlined),\n'
              '              label: const Text(\'记录新问题\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        if (!hasScheduledWork && !hasImmediateWork)\n'
              '          _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '            title: \'今天暂时没有需要处理的事项\',\n'
              '            message: recentStudents.isEmpty\n'
              '                ? \'新的任课学生或需要跟进的问题会出现在这里。\'\n'
              '                : \'可以回看最近学生，或在课堂中随手记下一条新情况。\',\n'
              '            icon: Icons.check_circle_outline,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        else if (hasScheduledWork)\n'
              '          _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '            key: const Key(\'workspace-today-work-section\'),\n'
              '            title: \'今天的工作\',\n'
              '            count: \'${overdue.length + today.length} 项\',\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                if (overdue.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '                  _WorkspaceSubheading(\n'
              '                    label: \'已逾期\',\n'
              '                    color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,\n'
              '                    icon: Icons.warning_amber_outlined,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  ..._buildActionRows(overdue, workspace),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '                if (today.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '                  if (overdue.isNotEmpty) const Divider(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '                  _WorkspaceSubheading(\n'
              '                    label: \'今天到期\',\n'
              '                    color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.secondary,\n'
              '                    icon: Icons.today_outlined,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  ..._buildActionRows(today, workspace),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        if (future.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '          if (hasBlockBeforeFuture) const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '            key: const Key(\'workspace-future-actions-section\'),\n'
              '            title: \'之后要处理\',\n'
              '            count: \'${future.length} 项\',\n'
              '            showTopDivider: true,\n'
              '            action: future.length > _todayPreviewLimit\n'
              '                ? TextButton(\n'
              '                    key: const Key(\'workspace-future-actions-toggle\'),\n'
              '                    onPressed: () => setState(\n'
              '                      () => _showAllFutureActions = !_showAllFutureActions,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    child: AnimatedSwitcher(\n'
              '                      duration: AppMotion.effectiveDuration(\n'
              '                        context,\n'
              '                        AppMotion.quick,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      child: Text(\n'
              '                        _showAllFutureActions ? \'收起\' : \'查看全部\',\n'
              '                        key: ValueKey<bool>(_showAllFutureActions),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  )\n'
              '                : null,\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              children: _buildActionRows(\n'
              '                _showAllFutureActions\n'
              '                    ? future\n'
              '                    : future.take(_todayPreviewLimit).toList(),\n'
              '                workspace,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        if (undated.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '          if (hasBlockBeforeUndated) const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '            key: const Key(\'workspace-undated-actions-section\'),\n'
              '            title: \'待安排\',\n'
              '            count: \'${undated.length} 项\',\n'
              '            showTopDivider: true,\n'
              '            action: undated.length > _todayPreviewLimit\n'
              '                ? TextButton(\n'
              '                    key: const Key(\'workspace-undated-actions-toggle\'),\n'
              '                    onPressed: () => setState(\n'
              '                      () => _showAllUndatedActions = !_showAllUndatedActions,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    child: AnimatedSwitcher(\n'
              '                      duration: AppMotion.effectiveDuration(\n'
              '                        context,\n'
              '                        AppMotion.quick,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      child: Text(\n'
              '                        _showAllUndatedActions ? \'收起\' : \'查看全部\',\n'
              '                        key: ValueKey<bool>(_showAllUndatedActions),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  )\n'
              '                : null,\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              children: _buildActionRows(\n'
              '                _showAllUndatedActions\n'
              '                    ? undated\n'
              '                    : undated.take(_todayPreviewLimit).toList(),\n'
              '                workspace,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        if (recentStudents.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '            title: \'最近学生\',\n'
              '            showTopDivider: true,\n'
              '            action: TextButton(\n'
              '              onPressed: () => _selectDestination(1),\n'
              '              child: const Text(\'查看全部\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                for (final student in recentStudents.take(5))\n'
              '                  _WorkspaceStudentRow(\n'
              '                    student: student,\n'
              '                    onOpen: () => _openStudent(student),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<WorkspaceActionWithContext> _actionsInBucket(\n'
              '    Iterable<WorkspaceActionWithContext> actions,\n'
              '    WorkspaceActionBucket bucket,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    return actions.where((item) => item.action.bucket == bucket).toList()\n'
              '      ..sort(_compareActionsForToday);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  int _compareActionsForToday(\n'
              '    WorkspaceActionWithContext left,\n'
              '    WorkspaceActionWithContext right,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    final leftDueAt = left.action.businessDueDate ?? left.action.dueAt;\n'
              '    final rightDueAt = right.action.businessDueDate ?? right.action.dueAt;\n'
              '    final dueAtComparison = switch ((leftDueAt, rightDueAt)) {\n'
              '      (final DateTime leftDate, final DateTime rightDate) => leftDate.compareTo(\n'
              '        rightDate,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '      (null, final DateTime _) => 1,\n'
              '      (final DateTime _, null) => -1,\n'
              '      (null, null) => 0,\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    if (dueAtComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return dueAtComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final studentComparison = left.student.name.compareTo(right.student.name);\n'
              '    if (studentComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return studentComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final subjectComparison = left.student.subject.compareTo(\n'
              '      right.student.subject,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (subjectComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return subjectComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final caseComparison = left.learningCase.title.compareTo(\n'
              '      right.learningCase.title,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (caseComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return caseComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return left.action.id.compareTo(right.action.id);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<WorkspaceStudent> _studentsByRecentActivity(\n'
              '    Iterable<WorkspaceStudent> students,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    return students.toList()..sort((left, right) {\n'
              '      final leftOccurredAt = _latestActivityAt(left);\n'
              '      final rightOccurredAt = _latestActivityAt(right);\n'
              '      final activityComparison = switch ((leftOccurredAt, rightOccurredAt)) {\n'
              '        (final DateTime leftDate, final DateTime rightDate) =>\n'
              '          rightDate.compareTo(leftDate),\n'
              '        (null, final DateTime _) => 1,\n'
              '        (final DateTime _, null) => -1,\n'
              '        (null, null) => 0,\n'
              '      };\n'
              '      if (activityComparison != 0) {\n'
              '        return activityComparison;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      final nameComparison = left.name.compareTo(right.name);\n'
              '      if (nameComparison != 0) {\n'
              '        return nameComparison;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final subjectComparison = left.subject.compareTo(right.subject);\n'
              '      if (subjectComparison != 0) {\n'
              '        return subjectComparison;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return left.profileId.compareTo(right.profileId);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  DateTime? _latestActivityAt(WorkspaceStudent student) {\n'
              '    DateTime? latest;\n'
              '    for (final fact in student.recentFacts) {\n'
              '      if (latest == null || fact.occurredAt.isAfter(latest)) {\n'
              '        latest = fact.occurredAt;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return latest;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<Widget> _buildActionRows(\n'
              '    List<WorkspaceActionWithContext> items,\n'
              '    TeacherWorkspace workspace,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    final grouped = <String, List<WorkspaceActionWithContext>>{};\n'
              '    for (final item in items) {\n'
              '      grouped.putIfAbsent(item.student.profileId, () => []).add(item);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return [\n'
              '      for (final group in grouped.values)\n'
              '        _WorkspaceActionGroup(\n'
              '          student: group.first.student,\n'
              '          items: group,\n'
              '          progressiveFlow: widget.progressiveCaseRepository != null,\n'
              '          onOpenCase: (learningCase) =>\n'
              '              _openCase(group.first.student, learningCase),\n'
              '          onReschedule: (item) => _rescheduleAction(workspace, item),\n'
              '          onComplete: (item) => _completeAction(workspace, item),\n'
              '          reschedulingActionId: _reschedulingActionId,\n'
              '          completingActionId: _completingActionId,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildStudents(TeacherWorkspace workspace) {\n'
              '    return AnimatedBuilder(\n'
              '      animation: _studentSearchController,\n'
              '      builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '        final query = _studentSearchController.text.trim();\n'
              '        final studentGroups = _groupStudentsById(workspace.students).where((\n'
              '          group,\n'
              '        ) {\n'
              '          if (query.isEmpty) {\n'
              '            return true;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          return group.any((student) {\n'
              '            final caseText = student.cases\n'
              '                .map(\n'
              '                  (learningCase) =>\n'
              '                      \'${learningCase.title}${learningCase.description ?? \'\'}\',\n'
              '                )\n'
              '                .join();\n'
              '            return \'${student.name}${student.subject}${student.context}$caseText\'\n'
              '                .contains(query);\n'
              '          });\n'
              '        }).toList();\n'
              '        return Column(\n'
              '          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            _WorkspacePageHeader(\n'
              '              title: \'学生\',\n'
              '              subtitle: \'搜索学生，先理解当前重点，再进入需要处理的问题。\',\n'
              '              actions: [\n'
              '                FilledButton.icon(\n'
              '                  onPressed: () => _showQuickCapture(),\n'
              '                  icon: Icon(Icons.edit_note_outlined),\n'
