              '                  label: const Text(\'记录新问题\'),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            TextField(\n'
              '              key: const Key(\'workspace-student-search\'),\n'
              '              controller: _studentSearchController,\n'
              '              decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                labelText: \'搜索学生或学情\',\n'
              '                hintText: \'输入姓名、学科或问题关键词\',\n'
              '                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),\n'
              '                suffixIcon: _studentSearchController.text.isEmpty\n'
              '                    ? null\n'
              '                    : IconButton(\n'
              '                        key: const Key(\'workspace-student-search-clear\'),\n'
              '                        tooltip: \'清空搜索\',\n'
              '                        onPressed: _studentSearchController.clear,\n'
              '                        icon: const Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              textInputAction: TextInputAction.search,\n'
              '              onSubmitted: (_) => FocusScope.of(context).unfocus(),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '            if (studentGroups.isEmpty)\n'
              '              const _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '                title: \'没有找到匹配的学生\',\n'
              '                message: \'换一个姓名、学科或问题关键词试试。\',\n'
              '                icon: Icons.search_off_outlined,\n'
              '              )\n'
              '            else\n'
              '              _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '                title: \'学生列表\',\n'
              '                count: \'${studentGroups.length} 人\',\n'
              '                child: Column(\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    for (final group in studentGroups)\n'
              '                      if (group.length == 1)\n'
              '                        _WorkspaceStudentRow(\n'
              '                          student: group.single,\n'
              '                          onOpen: () => _openStudent(group.single),\n'
              '                        )\n'
              '                      else\n'
              '                        _WorkspaceMultiSubjectStudentRow(\n'
              '                          students: group,\n'
              '                          onOpen: _openStudent,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _exportStudentSubject(WorkspaceStudent student) async {\n'
              '    late final List<LearningRecordExportRow> rows;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final recordRepository = widget.studentLearningRecordRepository;\n'
              '      rows = recordRepository == null\n'
              '          ? LearningRecordExport.rowsForStudentSubject(student)\n'
              '          : LearningRecordExport.rowsForStudentRecords(\n'
              '              await recordRepository.listStudentSubjectRecords(\n'
              '                profileId: student.profileId,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            );\n'
              '    } catch (error, stackTrace) {\n'
              '      _workspaceLogger.error(\n'
              '        \'student_subject_export_load_failed\',\n'
              '        error: error,\n'
              '        stackTrace: stackTrace,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        final message =\n'
              '            studentLearningRecordExportErrorMessage(error) ??\n'
              '            \'学情记录暂时无法读取，请检查网络后重试。\';\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (rows.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text(\'当前没有可导出的记录。\')));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final savedPath = await LearningRecordExport.saveAsXlsx(\n'
              '        fileNameWithoutExtension: LearningRecordExport.studentSubjectFileName(\n'
              '          student,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        rows: rows,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '        SnackBar(content: Text(savedPath == null ? \'已取消导出。\' : \'学情记录表已生成。\')),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (error, stackTrace) {\n'
              '      _workspaceLogger.error(\n'
              '        \'student_subject_export_failed\',\n'
              '        error: error,\n'
              '        stackTrace: stackTrace,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text(\'导出失败，请重试。\')));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildStudentDetail(\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent student,\n'
              '    WindowSizeClass sizeClass,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    final importantCases = _importantCasesForStudent(student);\n'
              '    final allCases = student.cases.toList()\n'
              '      ..sort(_compareCasesForStudentDetail);\n'
              '    final visibleCases = _showAllStudentCases ? allCases : importantCases;\n'
              '    final visibleCaseIds = visibleCases.map((item) => item.id).toSet();\n'
              '    final additionalPendingCases = _showAllStudentCases\n'
              '        ? const <WorkspaceCase>[]\n'
              '        : allCases\n'
              '              .where(\n'
              '                (learningCase) =>\n'
              '                    learningCase.status ==\n'
              '                        LearningCaseStatus.pendingVerification &&\n'
              '                    !visibleCaseIds.contains(learningCase.id),\n'
              '              )\n'
              '              .toList();\n'
              '    final pendingPreview = additionalPendingCases\n'
              '        .take(_studentPendingPreviewLimit)\n'
              '        .toList();\n'
              '    final hasAdditionalCases = allCases.length > importantCases.length;\n'
              '\n'
              '    return Column(\n'
              '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        _WorkspacePageHeader(\n'
              '          title: student.name,\n'
              '          subtitle:\n'
              '              \'${student.grade} · ${student.subject} · ${student.context}\',\n'
              '          leading: IconButton(\n'
              '            tooltip: \'返回\',\n'
              '            onPressed: _goBack,\n'
              '            icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_back),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          actions: [\n'
              '            OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
              '              key: const Key(\'student-detail-export\'),\n'
              '              onPressed: () => unawaited(_exportStudentSubject(student)),\n'
              '              icon: const Icon(Icons.download_outlined),\n'
              '              label: const Text(\'导出学情记录\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            FilledButton.icon(\n'
              '              onPressed: () => _showQuickCapture(student: student),\n'
              '              icon: Icon(Icons.edit_note_outlined),\n'
              '              label: const Text(\'记录新问题\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        if (student.positioning?.trim().isNotEmpty == true) ...[\n'
              '          Text(\n'
              '            student.positioning!,\n'
              '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(\n'
              '              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '          key: const Key(\'student-detail-focus-section\'),\n'
              '          title: _showAllStudentCases ? \'全部问题\' : \'现在最重要的事\',\n'
              '          count: \'${visibleCases.length} 项\',\n'
              '          action: hasAdditionalCases\n'
              '              ? TextButton(\n'
              '                  key: const Key(\'student-detail-cases-toggle\'),\n'
              '                  onPressed: () => setState(\n'
              '                    () => _showAllStudentCases = !_showAllStudentCases,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  child: Text(\n'
              '                    _showAllStudentCases ? \'只看重点\' : \'查看全部 ${allCases.length} 个\',\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                )\n'
              '              : null,\n'
              '          child: visibleCases.isEmpty\n'
              '              ? _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '                  title: allCases.isEmpty ? \'还没有记录的问题\' : \'当前没有需要跟进的问题\',\n'
              '                  message: allCases.isEmpty\n'
              '                      ? \'发现新的问题时，可以先记一句；已有问题有变化时，直接点“记进展”。\'\n'
              '                      : \'已有问题记录仍然保留，需要时可以查看全部。\',\n'
              '                  icon: Icons.inbox_outlined,\n'
              '                )\n'
              '              : Column(\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    for (final learningCase in visibleCases)\n'
              '                      _WorkspaceCaseRow(\n'
              '                        student: student,\n'
              '                        learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '                        onOpen: () => _openCase(student, learningCase),\n'
              '                        onRecordProgress:\n'
              '                            widget.progressiveCaseRepository != null &&\n'
              '                                learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.closed\n'
              '                            ? () => unawaited(\n'
              '                                _recordProgress(\n'
              '                                  student,\n'
              '                                  learningCase,\n'
              '                                  currentAction: learningCase.primaryAction,\n'
              '                                  preserveStudentOnly: true,\n'
              '                                ),\n'
              '                              )\n'
              '                            : null,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        if (additionalPendingCases.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '            key: const Key(\'student-detail-pending-section\'),\n'
              '            title: \'另外需要关注\',\n'
              '            count: \'${additionalPendingCases.length} 个\',\n'
              '            showTopDivider: true,\n'
              '            action: additionalPendingCases.length > _studentPendingPreviewLimit\n'
              '                ? TextButton(\n'
              '                    key: const Key(\'student-detail-pending-toggle\'),\n'
              '                    onPressed: () =>\n'
              '                        setState(() => _showAllStudentCases = true),\n'
              '                    child: const Text(\'查看全部\'),\n'
              '                  )\n'
              '                : null,\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                for (final learningCase in pendingPreview)\n'
              '                  _WorkspaceCaseRow(\n'
              '                    student: student,\n'
              '                    learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '                    onOpen: () => _openCase(student, learningCase),\n'
              '                    onRecordProgress:\n'
              '                        widget.progressiveCaseRepository != null &&\n'
              '                            learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.closed\n'
              '                        ? () => unawaited(\n'
              '                            _recordProgress(\n'
              '                              student,\n'
              '                              learningCase,\n'
              '                              currentAction: learningCase.primaryAction,\n'
              '                              preserveStudentOnly: true,\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          )\n'
              '                        : null,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '        _WorkspaceFacts(student: student, sizeClass: sizeClass),\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<WorkspaceCase> _importantCasesForStudent(WorkspaceStudent student) {\n'
              '    final cases =\n'
              '        student.cases\n'
              '            .where(\n'
              '              (learningCase) =>\n'
              '                  learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.closed,\n'
              '            )\n'
              '            .toList()\n'
              '          ..sort(_compareCasesForStudentDetail);\n'
              '    return cases.take(3).toList();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  int _compareCasesForStudentDetail(WorkspaceCase left, WorkspaceCase right) {\n'
              '    final attentionComparison = _caseAttentionRank(left)\n'
              '        .compareTo(_caseAttentionRank(right));\n'
              '    if (attentionComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return attentionComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final leftAction = left.primaryAction;\n'
              '    final rightAction = right.primaryAction;\n'
              '    final leftDueAt = leftAction?.businessDueDate ?? leftAction?.dueAt;\n'
              '    final rightDueAt = rightAction?.businessDueDate ?? rightAction?.dueAt;\n'
              '    final dueAtComparison = switch ((leftDueAt, rightDueAt)) {\n'
              '      (final DateTime leftDate, final DateTime rightDate) => leftDate.compareTo(\n'
              '        rightDate,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '      (null, final DateTime _) => 1,\n'
              '      (final DateTime _, null) => -1,\n'
              '      (null, null) => 0,\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    if (dueAtComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return dueAtComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final priorityComparison = _casePriorityRank(left.priority)\n'
              '        .compareTo(_casePriorityRank(right.priority));\n'
              '    if (priorityComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return priorityComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final observedAtComparison = left.firstObservedAt.compareTo(\n'
              '      right.firstObservedAt,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (observedAtComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return observedAtComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final titleComparison = left.title.compareTo(right.title);\n'
              '    if (titleComparison != 0) {\n'
              '      return titleComparison;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return left.id.compareTo(right.id);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  int _caseAttentionRank(WorkspaceCase learningCase) {\n'
              '    if (learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.pendingVerification) {\n'
              '      return 2;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final action = learningCase.primaryAction;\n'
              '    if (action != null) {\n'
              '      return switch (action.bucket) {\n'
              '        WorkspaceActionBucket.overdue => 0,\n'
              '        WorkspaceActionBucket.today => 1,\n'
              '        WorkspaceActionBucket.undated => 4,\n'
              '        WorkspaceActionBucket.future => 5,\n'
              '      };\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.newCase) {\n'
              '      return 3;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return 6;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  int _casePriorityRank(String priority) {\n'
              '    return switch (priority) {\n'
              '      \'high\' => 0,\n'
              '      \'low\' => 2,\n'
              '      _ => 1,\n'
              '    };\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildCaseDetail(\n'
              '    TeacherWorkspace workspace,\n'
              '    WorkspaceStudent student,\n'
              '    WorkspaceCase learningCase,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    final primaryAction = learningCase.primaryAction;\n'
              '    final useProgressiveFlow = widget.progressiveCaseRepository != null;\n'
              '    final commandLabel = useProgressiveFlow\n'
              '        ? null\n'
              '        : _caseCommandLabel(learningCase);\n'
              '    final canStabilize = !useProgressiveFlow && _canStabilizeCase(learningCase);\n'
              '    final visibleTimeline = _showAllCaseTimeline\n'
              '        ? learningCase.timeline\n'
              '        : learningCase.timeline.take(_caseTimelinePreviewLimit);\n'
              '    return Column(\n'
              '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        _WorkspacePageHeader(\n'
              '          title: learningCase.title,\n'
              '          subtitle: \'${student.name} · ${student.subject}\',\n'
              '          leading: IconButton(\n'
              '            tooltip: \'返回学生详情\',\n'
              '            onPressed: _goBack,\n'
              '            icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_back),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          actions: [_WorkspaceStatusMarker(label: learningCase.status.label)],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        Wrap(\n'
              '          spacing: AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '          runSpacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            _WorkspaceMetadata(learningCase.typeLabel),\n'
              '            _WorkspaceMetadata(_priorityLabel(learningCase.priority)),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '        _WorkspaceNarrativeSection(\n'
              '          title: useProgressiveFlow ? \'当前提醒\' : \'下一步\',\n'
              '          content: primaryAction == null\n'
              '              ? useProgressiveFlow\n'
              '                    ? \'当前没有待办；有新情况时直接记录进展。\'\n'
              '                    : \'当前没有待完成的主要行动。\'\n'
              '              : \'${primaryAction.title}（${_formatActionDate(primaryAction)}）\',\n'
              '          isPrimary: true,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        if (learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.pendingVerification)\n'
              '          _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '            title: useProgressiveFlow ? \'本次检查已经记录\' : \'本次验证通过，仍待确认是否稳定\',\n'
              '            message: useProgressiveFlow\n'
              '                ? \'可以继续观察、设置提醒，或者结束本轮跟进。\'\n'
              '                : \'本次检查已经通过，还需要你确认这个问题是否已经稳定。\',\n'
              '            icon: Icons.fact_check_outlined,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        else if (learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.stable)\n'
              '          _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '            title: useProgressiveFlow ? \'当前表现已经稳定\' : \'稳定；仍需安排下一次检查\',\n'
              '            message: useProgressiveFlow\n'
              '                ? \'可以继续观察，也可以直接结束本轮跟进。\'\n'
              '                : \'当前表现已经稳定，但还没有结束跟进；请保留下一次复查。\',\n'
              '            icon: Icons.check_circle_outline,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        else if (learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.newCase)\n'
              '          _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '            title: \'新记录\',\n'
              '            message: useProgressiveFlow\n'
              '                ? \'这是一条课堂快速记录；有新情况时继续记录，确认无需再跟进时可以直接结束。\'\n'
              '                : \'这是一条课堂快速记录；确认前请补充教师判断和合适的下一步。\',\n'
              '            icon: Icons.edit_note_outlined,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        if (useProgressiveFlow &&\n'
              '            learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.closed) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceCaseCommandSection(\n'
              '            title: \'继续理解这个问题\',\n'
              '            message: \'记录本次真实发生的情况；只有确实需要提醒自己时才设置下一步。\',\n'
              '            buttonLabel: \'记录进展\',\n'
              '            onPressed: () => unawaited(\n'
              '              _recordProgress(\n'
              '                student,\n'
              '                learningCase,\n'
              '                currentAction: primaryAction,\n'
              '                preserveSelection: true,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            secondaryButtonLabel: \'结束跟进\',\n'
              '            onSecondaryPressed: () => unawaited(\n'
              '              _endProgressiveFollowUp(\n'
              '                student,\n'
              '                learningCase,\n'
              '                preserveSelection: true,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        if (commandLabel != null) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceCaseCommandSection(\n'
              '            title: commandLabel,\n'
              '            message: _caseCommandHint(learningCase),\n'
              '            buttonLabel: commandLabel,\n'
              '            onPressed: () => _showCaseCommand(student, learningCase),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        if (canStabilize) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceCaseCommandSection(\n'
              '            title: \'确认已经稳定\',\n'
              '            message: \'最新验证结果为通过。确认稳定后会安排一次复查，问题历史会继续保留。\',\n'
              '            buttonLabel: \'标记为稳定\',\n'
              '            onPressed: () => _showStabilizeCase(student, learningCase),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        if (!useProgressiveFlow &&\n'
              '            learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.stable) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceCaseCommandSection(\n'
              '            title: \'结束跟进\',\n'
              '            message: \'确认这个问题已经稳定收口后再结束跟进；历史记录会完整保留。\',\n'
              '            buttonLabel: \'结束跟进\',\n'
              '            onPressed: () => _closeCase(student, learningCase),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        if (learningCase.status == LearningCaseStatus.closed) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceCaseCommandSection(\n'
              '            title: \'记录复发并继续跟进\',\n'
              '            message: \'结束跟进后如果再次出现新的表现，可以记录复发并继续跟进；之前的历史会完整保留。\',\n'
              '            buttonLabel: \'记录复发并继续跟进\',\n'
              '            onPressed: () => _showReopenCase(student, learningCase),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '        _WorkspaceNarrativeSection(\n'
              '          title: \'问题\',\n'
              '          content: learningCase.description ?? \'尚未补充问题说明。\',\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '          title: \'成长记录\',\n'
              '          count: learningCase.timeline.isEmpty\n'
              '              ? null\n'
              '              : \'${learningCase.timeline.length} 条\',\n'
              '          showTopDivider: true,\n'
              '          action: learningCase.timeline.length > _caseTimelinePreviewLimit\n'
              '              ? TextButton(\n'
              '                  key: const Key(\'workspace-case-timeline-toggle\'),\n'
              '                  onPressed: () => setState(\n'
              '                    () => _showAllCaseTimeline = !_showAllCaseTimeline,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  child: Text(_showAllCaseTimeline ? \'收起记录\' : \'查看更早记录\'),\n'
              '                )\n'
              '              : null,\n'
              '          child: learningCase.timeline.isEmpty\n'
              '              ? const _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '                  title: \'暂时没有更多历史\',\n'
              '                  message: \'新的课堂记录、教学处理和检查结果会按时间追加在这里。\',\n'
              '                  icon: Icons.history_outlined,\n'
              '                )\n'
              '              : _WorkspaceTimelineGroupedList(events: visibleTimeline),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '        Align(\n'
              '          alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,\n'
              '          child: TextButton.icon(\n'
              '            key: const Key(\'workspace-case-category-toggle\'),\n'
              '            onPressed: () => setState(\n'
              '              () => _showCaseCategoryDetails = !_showCaseCategoryDetails,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            icon: Icon(\n'
              '              _showCaseCategoryDetails\n'
              '                  ? Icons.expand_less\n'
              '                  : Icons.view_list_outlined,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            label: Text(_showCaseCategoryDetails ? \'收起分类记录\' : \'按类别查看与照片\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        if (_showCaseCategoryDetails) ...[\n'
              '          Text(\n'
              '            \'这里按类别核对学生表现、教学处理和检查结果；成长过程仍以时间顺序为主。\',\n'
              '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(\n'
              '              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceEvidenceSection(\n'
              '            learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '            organizationId: workspace.organizationId,\n'
              '            repository: widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceNarrativeSection(\n'
              '            title: \'教学处理\',\n'
              '            content: learningCase.interventions.isEmpty\n'
              '                ? \'尚未记录教学处理。\'\n'
              '                : learningCase.interventions\n'
              '                      .map(\n'
              '                        (item) =>\n'
              '                            \'${_formatDate(item.occurredAt)}：${item.strategy}\',\n'
              '                      )\n'
              '                      .join(\'\\n\\n\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceNarrativeSection(\n'
              '            title: \'检查结果\',\n'
              '            content: learningCase.assessments.isEmpty\n'
              '                ? \'尚未记录检查结果。\'\n'
              '                : learningCase.assessments\n'
              '                      .map(\n'
              '                        (item) =>\n'
              '                            \'${_formatDate(item.assessedAt)} ${_assessmentLabel(item.result)}：${item.evidenceSummary}\',\n'
              '                      )\n'
              '                      .join(\'\\n\\n\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceReopenCaseForm extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceReopenCaseForm({\n'
              '    required this.learningCase,\n'
              '    required this.repository,\n'
              '    required this.draftStore,\n'
              '    this.businessDate,\n'
              '    this.draftScopeKey,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceCase learningCase;\n'
              '  final LearningRepository repository;\n'
              '  final CaseReopenDraftStore draftStore;\n'
              '  final DateTime? businessDate;\n'
              '  final String? draftScopeKey;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_WorkspaceReopenCaseForm> createState() =>\n'
              '      _WorkspaceReopenCaseFormState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceReopenCaseFormState extends State<_WorkspaceReopenCaseForm> {\n'
              '  static const Map<String, String> _sourceTypeLabels = <String, String>{\n'
              '    \'observation\': \'课堂观察\',\n'
              '    \'homework\': \'作业\',\n'
              '    \'quiz\': \'小测\',\n'
              '    \'exam\': \'考试\',\n'
              '    \'essay\': \'作文\',\n'
              '    \'classwork\': \'课堂练习\',\n'
              '    \'guardian_report\': \'家长反馈\',\n'
              '    \'other\': \'其他\',\n'
              '  };\n'
              '\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _evidenceTitleController;\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _evidenceSummaryController;\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _nextActionController;\n'
              '  late String _evidenceOperationId;\n'
              '  late String _reopenOperationId;\n'
              '  late int _expectedCaseVersion;\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _sourceType = \'observation\';\n'
              '  CaseActionType _nextActionType = CaseActionType.verify;\n'
              '  DateTime _observedAt = DateTime.now();\n'
              '  DateTime? _nextActionDueOn;\n'
              '  String? _evidenceId;\n'
              '  int _evidenceVersion = 1;\n'
              '  String? _evidenceTitleError;\n'
              '  String? _evidenceSummaryError;\n'
              '  String? _nextActionError;\n'
              '  String? _saveError;\n'
              '  bool _submissionStarted = false;\n'
              '  bool _saving = false;\n'
              '  bool _restoring = false;\n'
              '  bool _draftStorageFailed = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  bool get _inputsLocked =>\n'
              '      _restoring || _draftStorageFailed || _submissionStarted;\n'
              '  bool get _isDirty =>\n'
              '      _submissionStarted ||\n'
              '      _evidenceTitleController.text.trim().isNotEmpty ||\n'
              '      _evidenceSummaryController.text.trim().isNotEmpty ||\n'
              '      _nextActionController.text.trim() != \'复发后安排验证\' ||\n'
              '      _nextActionDueOn != null ||\n'
              '      _sourceType != \'observation\';\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _evidenceOperationId = createOperationId();\n'
              '    _reopenOperationId = createOperationId();\n'
              '    _expectedCaseVersion = widget.learningCase.version;\n'
              '    _evidenceTitleController = TextEditingController();\n'
              '    _evidenceSummaryController = TextEditingController();\n'
              '    _nextActionController = TextEditingController(text: \'复发后安排验证\');\n'
              '    _evidenceTitleController.addListener(_clearInlineErrors);\n'
              '    _evidenceSummaryController.addListener(_clearInlineErrors);\n'
              '    _nextActionController.addListener(_clearInlineErrors);\n'
              '    _restoring = widget.draftScopeKey != null;\n'
              '    if (_restoring) {\n'
              '      unawaited(_restoreDraft());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _restoreDraft() async {\n'
              '    final scopeKey = widget.draftScopeKey;\n'
              '    if (scopeKey == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final draft = await widget.draftStore.load(scopeKey);\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (draft != null) {\n'
              '        if (draft.caseId != widget.learningCase.id) {\n'
              '          throw const FormatException(\n'
              '            \'Persisted case reopen draft targets a different Case.\',\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        _evidenceOperationId = draft.evidenceOperationId;\n'
              '        _reopenOperationId = draft.reopenOperationId;\n'
              '        _expectedCaseVersion = draft.expectedCaseVersion;\n'
              '        _sourceType = draft.sourceType;\n'
              '        _evidenceTitleController.text = draft.evidenceTitle;\n'
              '        _evidenceSummaryController.text = draft.evidenceSummary;\n'
              '        _observedAt = draft.observedAt;\n'
              '        _evidenceId = draft.evidenceId;\n'
              '        _evidenceVersion = draft.evidenceVersion;\n'
              '        _nextActionType = CaseActionType.values.firstWhere(\n'
              '          (type) => type.wireValue == draft.nextActionTypeWire,\n'
              '          orElse: () => CaseActionType.other,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        _nextActionController.text = draft.nextActionTitle;\n'
              '        _nextActionDueOn = draft.nextActionDueOn;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _restoring = false;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _restoring = false;\n'
              '        _draftStorageFailed = true;\n'
              '        _saveError = \'无法安全恢复未完成的复发记录。为避免重复提交，暂不能保存；可以关闭窗口后重试。\';\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  CaseReopenDraft _draftFromForm() {\n'
              '    return CaseReopenDraft(\n'
              '      schemaVersion: CaseReopenDraft.currentSchemaVersion,\n'
              '      caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '      expectedCaseVersion: _expectedCaseVersion,\n'
              '      evidenceOperationId: _evidenceOperationId,\n'
              '      reopenOperationId: _reopenOperationId,\n'
              '      sourceType: _sourceType,\n'
              '      evidenceTitle: _evidenceTitleController.text.trim(),\n'
              '      evidenceSummary: _evidenceSummaryController.text.trim(),\n'
              '      observedAt: _observedAt,\n'
              '      evidenceId: _evidenceId,\n'
              '      evidenceVersion: _evidenceVersion,\n'
              '      nextActionTypeWire: _nextActionType.wireValue,\n'
              '      nextActionTitle: _nextActionController.text.trim(),\n'
              '      nextActionDueOn: _nextActionDueOn,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _persistDraft() async {\n'
              '    final scopeKey = widget.draftScopeKey;\n'
              '    if (scopeKey == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await widget.draftStore.save(scopeKey, _draftFromForm());\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _clearPersistedDraft() async {\n'
              '    final scopeKey = widget.draftScopeKey;\n'
              '    if (scopeKey == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await widget.draftStore.clear(scopeKey);\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      // A committed reopen is safe to retry with the same operation ID.\n'
              '      // Keeping the draft is safer than masking a successful server result.\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _evidenceTitleController\n'
              '      ..removeListener(_clearInlineErrors)\n'
              '      ..dispose();\n'
              '    _evidenceSummaryController\n'
              '      ..removeListener(_clearInlineErrors)\n'
              '      ..dispose();\n'
              '    _nextActionController\n'
              '      ..removeListener(_clearInlineErrors)\n'
              '      ..dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _clearInlineErrors() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final titleReady =\n'
              '        _evidenceTitleError != null &&\n'
              '        _evidenceTitleController.text.trim().isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    final summaryReady =\n'
              '        _evidenceSummaryError != null &&\n'
              '        _evidenceSummaryController.text.trim().isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    final actionReady =\n'
              '        _nextActionError != null &&\n'
              '        _nextActionController.text.trim().isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    if (titleReady || summaryReady || actionReady) {\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        if (titleReady) {\n'
              '          _evidenceTitleError = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (summaryReady) {\n'
              '          _evidenceSummaryError = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (actionReady) {\n'
              '          _nextActionError = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _pickObservedAt() async {\n'
              '    if (_inputsLocked) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final pickedDate = await showDatePicker(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      initialDate: DateTime(\n'
              '        _observedAt.year,\n'
              '        _observedAt.month,\n'
              '        _observedAt.day,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '      firstDate: DateTime(2000),\n'
              '      lastDate: DateTime(now.year + 2, 12, 31),\n'
              '      helpText: \'选择实际观察日期\',\n'
              '      cancelText: \'取消\',\n'
              '      confirmText: \'确定\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || pickedDate == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final pickedTime = await showTimePicker(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      initialTime: TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(_observedAt),\n'
              '      helpText: \'选择实际观察时间\',\n'
              '      cancelText: \'取消\',\n'
              '      confirmText: \'确定\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || pickedTime == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _observedAt = DateTime(\n'
              '        pickedDate.year,\n'
              '        pickedDate.month,\n'
              '        pickedDate.day,\n'
              '        pickedTime.hour,\n'
              '        pickedTime.minute,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _pickDueDate() async {\n'
              '    if (_inputsLocked) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final businessNow = widget.businessDate ?? DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final today = DateTime(\n'
              '      businessNow.year,\n'
              '      businessNow.month,\n'
              '      businessNow.day,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final selected = await showDatePicker(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      initialDate: _nextActionDueOn ?? today,\n'
              '      firstDate: today,\n'
              '      lastDate: DateTime(today.year + 2, 12, 31),\n'
              '      helpText: \'选择下一行动日期\',\n'
              '      cancelText: \'取消\',\n'
              '      confirmText: \'确定\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || selected == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _nextActionDueOn = DateTime(selected.year, selected.month, selected.day);\n'
              '      if (_nextActionType == CaseActionType.review) {\n'
              '        _nextActionError = null;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _save() async {\n'
              '    if (_saving || _restoring || _draftStorageFailed) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final title = _evidenceTitleController.text.trim();\n'
              '    final summary = _evidenceSummaryController.text.trim();\n'
              '    final nextActionTitle = _nextActionController.text.trim();\n'
              '    var valid = true;\n'
              '    if (title.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      _evidenceTitleError = \'请写下这次复发证据的标题\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (summary.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      _evidenceSummaryError = \'请写下可观察到的复发表现\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (nextActionTitle.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      _nextActionError = \'请保留或改写下一行动\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_nextActionType == CaseActionType.review && _nextActionDueOn == null) {\n'
              '      _nextActionError = \'复查行动需要安排日期\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!valid) {\n'
              '      setState(() {});\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _persistDraft();\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _saveError = \'无法安全保存恢复记录，未提交到服务器。请重试或关闭窗口后再试。\';\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _submissionStarted = true;\n'
              '      _saving = true;\n'
              '      _saveError = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      if (_evidenceId == null) {\n'
              '        final evidenceReceipt = await widget.repository.addCaseEvidence(\n'
              '          AddCaseEvidenceCommand(\n'
              '            operationId: _evidenceOperationId,\n'
              '            caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '            expectedCaseVersion: _expectedCaseVersion,\n'
              '            sourceType: _sourceType,\n'
              '            title: title,\n'
              '            observedAt: _observedAt,\n'
              '            summary: summary,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        final evidenceId = evidenceReceipt.recordId;\n'
              '        if (evidenceId == null || evidenceId.trim().isEmpty) {\n'
              '          throw const FormatException(\n'
              '            \'add_case_evidence returned no evidence id.\',\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (!mounted) {\n'
              '          return;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _evidenceId = evidenceId;\n'
              '          _evidenceVersion = 1;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          await _persistDraft();\n'
              '        } catch (error) {\n'
              '          throw _CaseReopenDraftStorageException(error);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final evidenceId = _evidenceId;\n'
              '      if (evidenceId == null) {\n'
              '        throw const FormatException(\'Missing committed Evidence id.\');\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final receipt = await widget.repository.reopenCase(\n'
              '        ReopenCaseCommand(\n'
              '          operationId: _reopenOperationId,\n'
              '          caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '          expectedCaseVersion: _expectedCaseVersion,\n'
              '          recurrenceEvidenceIds: <String>[evidenceId],\n'
              '          expectedEvidenceVersions: <String, int>{evidenceId: _evidenceVersion},\n'
              '          nextActionType: _nextActionType,\n'
              '          nextActionTitle: nextActionTitle,\n'
              '          nextActionDueOn: _nextActionDueOn,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      await _clearPersistedDraft();\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop(receipt);\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final hasCommittedEvidence = _evidenceId != null;\n'
              '      final unknownResult = _isUnknownResultFailure(error);\n'
              '      final saveError = error is _CaseReopenDraftStorageException\n'
              '          ? hasCommittedEvidence\n'
              '                ? \'这次观察已保存，但跟进状态暂时没有恢复。请保持页面打开并重试。\'\n'
              '                : \'这次内容还没有提交成功，请重试。\'\n'
              '          : _describeCaseCommandError(error);\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _saving = false;\n'
              '        _saveError = saveError;\n'
              '        if (!unknownResult && !hasCommittedEvidence) {\n'
              '          _submissionStarted = false;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _confirmDiscard() async {\n'
              '    if (_submissionStarted || _saving || _restoring) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!_isDirty) {\n'
              '      await _clearPersistedDraft();\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        Navigator.of(context).pop();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final discard = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '        title: const Text(\'放弃这次复发记录？\'),\n'
              '        content: const Text(\'当前输入还没有保存。放弃后不会新增这次观察记录。\'),\n'
              '        actions: [\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'继续编辑\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          FilledButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'放弃记录\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (mounted && discard == true) {\n'
              '      await _clearPersistedDraft();\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        Navigator.of(context).pop();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final bottomInset = MediaQuery.viewInsetsOf(context).bottom;\n'
              '    final caseContext =\n'
              '        \'${widget.learningCase.typeLabel} · ${widget.learningCase.status.label}\';\n'
              '    return PopScope<void>(\n'
              '      canPop: !_restoring && !_submissionStarted && !_saving,\n'
              '      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {\n'
              '        if (!didPop && !_restoring && !_submissionStarted && !_saving) {\n'
              '          _confirmDiscard();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      child: SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: AnimatedPadding(\n'
              '          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),\n'
              '          padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: bottomInset),\n'
              '          child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '            child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
              '              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 560),\n'
              '              child: Column(\n'
              '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                children: [\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: Text(\n'
              '                          \'记录复发并继续跟进\',\n'
              '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      IconButton(\n'
              '                        tooltip: \'关闭\',\n'
              '                        onPressed: _restoring || _submissionStarted || _saving\n'
              '                            ? null\n'
              '                            : _confirmDiscard,\n'
              '                        icon: Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Text(\n'
              '                    \'未完成的内容会自动保留；如果网络中断，重新打开后可以继续，不需要重复填写。\',\n'
              '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(\n'
              '                      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  _WorkspaceContextLine(label: \'当前问题\', value: caseContext),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  if (_restoring) const Text(\'正在恢复未完成的复发记录…\'),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  DropdownButtonFormField<String>(\n'
              '                    initialValue: _sourceType,\n'
              '                    decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: \'记录来源 *\'),\n'
              '                    items: [\n'
              '                      for (final entry in _sourceTypeLabels.entries)\n'
              '                        DropdownMenuItem<String>(\n'
              '                          value: entry.key,\n'
              '                          child: Text(entry.value),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                    onChanged: _inputsLocked\n'
              '                        ? null\n'
              '                        : (value) {\n'
              '                            if (value != null) {\n'
              '                              setState(() => _sourceType = value);\n'
              '                            }\n'
              '                          },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  TextField(\n'
              '                    key: const Key(\'reopen-evidence-title\'),\n'
              '                    controller: _evidenceTitleController,\n'
              '                    autofocus: true,\n'
              '                    enabled: !_inputsLocked,\n'
              '                    decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                      labelText: \'简要标题 *\',\n'
              '                      hintText: \'例如：关闭后再次跳过通分步骤\',\n'
              '                      errorText: _evidenceTitleError,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  TextField(\n'
              '                    key: const Key(\'reopen-evidence-summary\'),\n'
              '                    controller: _evidenceSummaryController,\n'
              '                    enabled: !_inputsLocked,\n'
              '                    minLines: 3,\n'
              '                    maxLines: 6,\n'
              '                    textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,\n'
              '                    decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                      labelText: \'具体表现 *\',\n'
              '                      hintText: \'写下这次实际看到的复发，而不是只写“又出现了”\',\n'
              '                      errorText: _evidenceSummaryError,\n'
              '                      alignLabelWithHint: true,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
              '                    onPressed: _inputsLocked ? null : _pickObservedAt,\n'
              '                    icon: Icon(Icons.schedule_outlined),\n'
              '                    label: Text(\n'
              '                      \'观察时间：${_formatDateTimeForReopen(_observedAt)}\',\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  DropdownButtonFormField<CaseActionType>(\n'
              '                    initialValue: _nextActionType,\n'
              '                    decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: \'下一步类型\'),\n'
              '                    items: [\n'
              '                      for (final type in CaseActionType.values)\n'
              '                        DropdownMenuItem<CaseActionType>(\n'
              '                          value: type,\n'
              '                          child: Text(type.label),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                    onChanged: _inputsLocked\n'
              '                        ? null\n'
              '                        : (value) {\n'
              '                            if (value != null) {\n'
              '                              setState(() {\n'
              '                                _nextActionType = value;\n'
              '                                if (value != CaseActionType.review) {\n'
              '                                  _nextActionError = null;\n'
              '                                }\n'
              '                              });\n'
              '                            }\n'
              '                          },\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  TextField(\n'
              '                    key: const Key(\'reopen-next-action\'),\n'
              '                    controller: _nextActionController,\n'
              '                    enabled: !_inputsLocked,\n'
              '                    textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,\n'
              '                    decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                      labelText: \'接下来的行动 *\',\n'
              '                      hintText: \'例如：复核复发原因并安排验证\',\n'
              '                      errorText: _nextActionError,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
              '                          onPressed: _inputsLocked ? null : _pickDueDate,\n'
              '                          icon: Icon(Icons.event_outlined),\n'
              '                          label: Text(\n'
              '                            _nextActionDueOn == null\n'
              '                                ? _nextActionType == CaseActionType.review\n'
              '                                      ? \'安排日期（必选）\'\n'
              '                                      : \'安排日期（可选）\'\n'
              '                                : \'行动日期：${_formatDateOnly(_nextActionDueOn!)}\',\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      if (_nextActionDueOn != null) ...[\n'
              '                        const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          tooltip: \'清除日期\',\n'
              '                          onPressed: _inputsLocked\n'
              '                              ? null\n'
              '                              : () => setState(() => _nextActionDueOn = null),\n'
              '                          icon: Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ],\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Text(\n'
              '                    \'观察时间需要晚于上次结束跟进；开始保存后请等待完成，网络异常时可以直接重试。\',\n'
              '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  if (_evidenceId != null) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      \'这次观察已经保存，继续提交即可恢复跟进；如果刚才网络中断，直接重试即可。\',\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(\n'
              '                        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  if (_saveError != null) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                    _WorkspaceErrorText(message: _saveError!),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: OutlinedButton(\n'
              '                          onPressed: _restoring || _submissionStarted || _saving\n'
              '                              ? null\n'
              '                              : _confirmDiscard,\n'
              '                          child: const Text(\'取消\'),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: FilledButton(\n'
              '                          onPressed:\n'
              '                              _saving || _restoring || _draftStorageFailed\n'
              '                              ? null\n'
              '                              : _save,\n'
              '                          child: Text(\n'
              '                            _saving\n'
              '                                ? \'保存中…\'\n'
              '                                : _evidenceId == null\n'
              '                                ? \'保存并继续\'\n'
              '                                : \'继续跟进\',\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'enum _CaseCommandMode { confirm, intervention, assessment, stabilize }\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCaseCommandForm extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceCaseCommandForm({\n'
              '    required this.mode,\n'
              '    required this.learningCase,\n'
              '    required this.repository,\n'
              '    this.businessDate,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final _CaseCommandMode mode;\n'
              '  final WorkspaceCase learningCase;\n'
              '  final LearningRepository repository;\n'
              '  final DateTime? businessDate;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_WorkspaceCaseCommandForm> createState() =>\n'
              '      _WorkspaceCaseCommandFormState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCaseCommandFormState extends State<_WorkspaceCaseCommandForm> {\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _strategyController;\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _evidenceController;\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _notesController;\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _nextActionController;\n'
              '  late final String _operationId;\n'
              '\n'
              '  CaseAssessmentResult _assessmentResult = CaseAssessmentResult.partial;\n'
              '  DateTime? _nextActionDueAt;\n'
              '  String? _strategyError;\n'
              '  String? _evidenceError;\n'
              '  String? _nextActionError;\n'
              '  String? _saveError;\n'
              '  bool _saving = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  String get _defaultNextActionTitle => switch (widget.mode) {\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.confirm => \'安排一次针对性练习\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.intervention => \'安排一次检查\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.assessment => \'安排下一次验证\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.stabilize => \'安排一次复查\',\n'
              '  };\n'
              '\n'
              '  String get _title => switch (widget.mode) {\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.confirm => \'整理并确认问题\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.intervention => \'记录教学动作\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.assessment => \'记录验证结果\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.stabilize => \'确认已经稳定\',\n'
              '  };\n'
              '\n'
              '  String get _subtitle => switch (widget.mode) {\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.confirm => \'把原始观察转成一个可执行的学习问题，并安排下一步。\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.intervention => \'记录这次实际做了什么，并明确下一次要检查什么。\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.assessment => \'记录本次检查看到的结果；不要用结果直接替代后续教师判断。\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.stabilize => \'最新验证已经通过；确认稳定后会安排一次复查，不会删除历史记录。\',\n'
              '  };\n'
              '\n'
              '  bool get _isDirty =>\n'
              '      _strategyController.text.trim().isNotEmpty ||\n'
              '      _evidenceController.text.trim().isNotEmpty ||\n'
              '      _notesController.text.trim().isNotEmpty ||\n'
              '      _nextActionController.text.trim() != _defaultNextActionTitle ||\n'
              '      _nextActionDueAt != null ||\n'
              '      _assessmentResult != CaseAssessmentResult.partial;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _operationId = createOperationId();\n'
              '    _strategyController = TextEditingController();\n'
              '    _evidenceController = TextEditingController();\n'
              '    _notesController = TextEditingController();\n'
              '    _nextActionController = TextEditingController(\n'
              '      text: _defaultNextActionTitle,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    _strategyController.addListener(_clearInlineErrors);\n'
              '    _evidenceController.addListener(_clearInlineErrors);\n'
              '    _nextActionController.addListener(_clearInlineErrors);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _strategyController\n'
              '      ..removeListener(_clearInlineErrors)\n'
              '      ..dispose();\n'
              '    _evidenceController\n'
              '      ..removeListener(_clearInlineErrors)\n'
              '      ..dispose();\n'
              '    _notesController.dispose();\n'
              '    _nextActionController\n'
              '      ..removeListener(_clearInlineErrors)\n'
              '      ..dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _clearInlineErrors() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final clearStrategy =\n'
              '        _strategyError != null && _strategyController.text.trim().isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    final clearEvidence =\n'
              '        _evidenceError != null && _evidenceController.text.trim().isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    final clearNextAction =\n'
              '        _nextActionError != null &&\n'
              '        _nextActionController.text.trim().isNotEmpty;\n'
              '    if (clearStrategy || clearEvidence || clearNextAction) {\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        if (clearStrategy) {\n'
              '          _strategyError = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (clearEvidence) {\n'
              '          _evidenceError = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (clearNextAction) {\n'
              '          _nextActionError = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _pickDueDate() async {\n'
              '    final businessNow = widget.businessDate ?? DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final today = DateTime(\n'
              '      businessNow.year,\n'
              '      businessNow.month,\n'
              '      businessNow.day,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final current = _nextActionDueAt ?? today;\n'
              '    final selected = await showDatePicker(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      initialDate: DateTime(current.year, current.month, current.day),\n'
              '      firstDate: DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day),\n'
              '      lastDate: DateTime(today.year + 2, 12, 31),\n'
              '      helpText: \'选择下一行动日期\',\n'
              '      cancelText: \'取消\',\n'
              '      confirmText: \'确定\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (selected == null || !mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      // Use UTC noon as the date-only convention; the read model renders\n'
              '      // the stored instant in the organization timezone.\n'
              '      _nextActionDueAt = DateTime.utc(\n'
              '        selected.year,\n'
              '        selected.month,\n'
              '        selected.day,\n'
              '        12,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _save() async {\n'
              '    if (_saving) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final strategy = _strategyController.text.trim();\n'
              '    final evidenceSummary = _evidenceController.text.trim();\n'
              '    final notes = _notesController.text.trim();\n'
              '    final nextActionTitle = _nextActionController.text.trim();\n'
              '\n'
              '    var valid = true;\n'
              '    if (widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.intervention && strategy.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      _strategyError = \'请写下这次实际采用的教学动作\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.assessment && evidenceSummary.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      _evidenceError = \'请写下本次验证中可观察到的结果\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (nextActionTitle.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      _nextActionError = \'请保留或改写下一行动\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!valid) {\n'
              '      setState(() {});\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _saving = true;\n'
              '      _saveError = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      late final CaseCommandReceipt receipt;\n'
              '      if (widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.confirm) {\n'
              '        receipt = await widget.repository.confirmCase(\n'
              '          ConfirmCaseCommand(\n'
              '            operationId: _operationId,\n'
              '            caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '            expectedCaseVersion: widget.learningCase.version,\n'
              '            nextActionTitle: nextActionTitle,\n'
              '            nextActionDueAt: _nextActionDueAt,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      } else if (widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.intervention) {\n'
              '        receipt = await widget.repository.recordIntervention(\n'
              '          RecordInterventionCommand(\n'
              '            operationId: _operationId,\n'
              '            caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '            expectedCaseVersion: widget.learningCase.version,\n'
              '            strategy: strategy,\n'
              '            notes: notes.isEmpty ? null : notes,\n'
              '            occurredAt: null,\n'
              '            nextActionTitle: nextActionTitle,\n'
              '            nextActionDueAt: _nextActionDueAt,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      } else if (widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.stabilize) {\n'
              '        receipt = await widget.repository.stabilizeCase(\n'
              '          StabilizeCaseCommand(\n'
              '            operationId: _operationId,\n'
              '            caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '            expectedCaseVersion: widget.learningCase.version,\n'
              '            stabilizedAt: null,\n'
              '            nextActionTitle: nextActionTitle,\n'
              '            nextActionDueAt: _nextActionDueAt,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        receipt = await widget.repository.recordAssessment(\n'
              '          RecordAssessmentCommand(\n'
              '            operationId: _operationId,\n'
              '            caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '            expectedCaseVersion: widget.learningCase.version,\n'
              '            result: _assessmentResult,\n'
              '            evidenceSummary: evidenceSummary,\n'
              '            notes: notes.isEmpty ? null : notes,\n'
              '            assessedAt: null,\n'
              '            nextActionTitle: nextActionTitle,\n'
              '            nextActionDueAt: _nextActionDueAt,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop(receipt);\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _saving = false;\n'
              '        _saveError = _describeCaseCommandError(error);\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _confirmDiscard() async {\n'
              '    if (_saving) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!_isDirty) {\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop();\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final discard = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '        title: const Text(\'放弃这次记录？\'),\n'
              '        content: const Text(\'当前输入还没有保存。放弃后不会生成新的教学事实。\'),\n'
              '        actions: [\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'继续编辑\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          FilledButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'放弃记录\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (mounted && discard == true) {\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final bottomInset = MediaQuery.viewInsetsOf(context).bottom;\n'
              '    final caseContext =\n'
              '        \'${widget.learningCase.typeLabel} · ${widget.learningCase.status.label}\';\n'
              '    return PopScope<void>(\n'
              '      canPop: !_isDirty && !_saving,\n'
              '      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {\n'
              '        if (!didPop && !_saving) {\n'
              '          _confirmDiscard();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      child: SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: AnimatedPadding(\n'
              '          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),\n'
