              '          padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: bottomInset),\n'
              '          child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '            child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
              '              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 560),\n'
              '              child: Column(\n'
              '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                children: [\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: Text(\n'
              '                          _title,\n'
              '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      IconButton(\n'
              '                        tooltip: \'关闭\',\n'
              '                        onPressed: _saving ? null : _confirmDiscard,\n'
              '                        icon: Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Text(\n'
              '                    _subtitle,\n'
              '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(\n'
              '                      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  _WorkspaceContextLine(label: \'当前问题\', value: caseContext),\n'
              '                  if (widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.intervention) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                    TextField(\n'
              '                      controller: _strategyController,\n'
              '                      autofocus: true,\n'
              '                      enabled: !_saving,\n'
              '                      minLines: 3,\n'
              '                      maxLines: 6,\n'
              '                      textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,\n'
              '                      decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                        labelText: \'这次怎么处理 *\',\n'
              '                        hintText: \'记下讲解、练习、提示或调整方式\',\n'
              '                        errorText: _strategyError,\n'
              '                        alignLabelWithHint: true,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  if (widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.assessment) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                    DropdownButtonFormField<CaseAssessmentResult>(\n'
              '                      initialValue: _assessmentResult,\n'
              '                      decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: \'验证结果 *\'),\n'
              '                      items: [\n'
              '                        for (final result in CaseAssessmentResult.values)\n'
              '                          DropdownMenuItem<CaseAssessmentResult>(\n'
              '                            value: result,\n'
              '                            child: Text(result.label),\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                      ],\n'
              '                      onChanged: _saving\n'
              '                          ? null\n'
              '                          : (result) {\n'
              '                              if (result != null) {\n'
              '                                setState(() => _assessmentResult = result);\n'
              '                              }\n'
              '                            },\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                    TextField(\n'
              '                      controller: _evidenceController,\n'
              '                      autofocus: true,\n'
              '                      enabled: !_saving,\n'
              '                      minLines: 3,\n'
              '                      maxLines: 6,\n'
              '                      textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,\n'
              '                      decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                        labelText: \'本次验证表现 *\',\n'
              '                        hintText: \'记下学生这次能否独立完成、错在哪里、是否需要提示\',\n'
              '                        errorText: _evidenceError,\n'
              '                        alignLabelWithHint: true,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  if (widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.intervention ||\n'
              '                      widget.mode == _CaseCommandMode.assessment) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                    TextField(\n'
              '                      controller: _notesController,\n'
              '                      enabled: !_saving,\n'
              '                      minLines: 2,\n'
              '                      maxLines: 4,\n'
              '                      textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,\n'
              '                      decoration: const InputDecoration(\n'
              '                        labelText: \'补充备注（可选）\',\n'
              '                        hintText: \'记录对下一次教学有帮助的上下文\',\n'
              '                        alignLabelWithHint: true,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  TextField(\n'
              '                    controller: _nextActionController,\n'
              '                    enabled: !_saving,\n'
              '                    textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,\n'
              '                    decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                      labelText: \'下一行动 *\',\n'
              '                      hintText: \'明确下一次要做什么\',\n'
              '                      errorText: _nextActionError,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
              '                          onPressed: _saving ? null : _pickDueDate,\n'
              '                          icon: Icon(Icons.event_outlined),\n'
              '                          label: Text(\n'
              '                            _nextActionDueAt == null\n'
              '                                ? \'安排日期（可选）\'\n'
              '                                : \'行动日期：${_formatDateOnly(_nextActionDueAt!)}\',\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      if (_nextActionDueAt != null) ...[\n'
              '                        const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          tooltip: \'清除日期\',\n'
              '                          onPressed: _saving\n'
              '                              ? null\n'
              '                              : () => setState(() => _nextActionDueAt = null),\n'
              '                          icon: Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ],\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Text(\n'
              '                    \'日期按机构时区计算；提交失败时输入会保留，直接重试即可，不会重复记录。\',\n'
              '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  if (_saveError != null) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                    _WorkspaceErrorText(message: _saveError!),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: OutlinedButton(\n'
              '                          onPressed: _saving ? null : _confirmDiscard,\n'
              '                          child: const Text(\'取消\'),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: FilledButton(\n'
              '                          onPressed: _saving ? null : _save,\n'
              '                          child: Text(_saving ? \'保存中…\' : \'保存并进入下一步\'),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceQuickCaptureForm extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceQuickCaptureForm({\n'
              '    required this.students,\n'
              '    required this.caseTypes,\n'
              '    required this.repository,\n'
              '    this.organizationId,\n'
              '    this.evidenceAttachmentRepository,\n'
              '    this.initialStudent,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final List<WorkspaceStudent> students;\n'
              '  final List<WorkspaceCaseType> caseTypes;\n'
              '  final WorkspaceStudent? initialStudent;\n'
              '  final LearningRepository repository;\n'
              '  final String? organizationId;\n'
              '  final EvidenceAttachmentRepository? evidenceAttachmentRepository;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_WorkspaceQuickCaptureForm> createState() =>\n'
              '      _WorkspaceQuickCaptureFormState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceQuickCaptureFormState\n'
              '    extends State<_WorkspaceQuickCaptureForm> {\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _titleController;\n'
              '  late final FocusNode _titleFocusNode;\n'
              '  late final String _operationId;\n'
              '  WorkspaceStudent? _selectedStudent;\n'
              '  String _selectedCaseTypeKey = WorkspaceCaseType.builtInTypes.last.key;\n'
              '  String? _studentError;\n'
              '  String? _titleError;\n'
              '  String? _attachmentError;\n'
              '  String? _saveError;\n'
              '  bool _saving = false;\n'
              '  bool _showMoreOptions = false;\n'
              '  PickedEvidenceAttachment? _selectedAttachment;\n'
              '\n'
              '  bool get _isDirty =>\n'
              '      _titleController.text.trim().isNotEmpty ||\n'
              '      _selectedAttachment != null ||\n'
              '      _selectedCaseTypeKey != WorkspaceCaseType.builtInTypes.last.key;\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<WorkspaceCaseType> get _caseTypeOptions {\n'
              '    final customTypes = widget.caseTypes.where(\n'
              '      (caseType) => !caseType.isBuiltIn && caseType.isActive,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return <WorkspaceCaseType>[\n'
              '      ...WorkspaceCaseType.builtInTypes,\n'
              '      ...customTypes,\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  WorkspaceCaseType get _selectedCaseType {\n'
              '    for (final caseType in _caseTypeOptions) {\n'
              '      if (caseType.key == _selectedCaseTypeKey) {\n'
              '        return caseType;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return WorkspaceCaseType.builtInTypes.last;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<WorkspaceCase> get _currentCasesForSelectedStudent {\n'
              '    final student = _selectedStudent;\n'
              '    if (student == null) {\n'
              '      return const <WorkspaceCase>[];\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final cases =\n'
              '        student.cases\n'
              '            .where(\n'
              '              (learningCase) =>\n'
              '                  learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.closed,\n'
              '            )\n'
              '            .toList()\n'
              '          ..sort(\n'
              '            (left, right) =>\n'
              '                right.firstObservedAt.compareTo(left.firstObservedAt),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '    return List<WorkspaceCase>.unmodifiable(cases);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _selectedStudent =\n'
              '        widget.initialStudent ??\n'
              '        (widget.students.length == 1 ? widget.students.first : null);\n'
              '    _operationId = createOperationId();\n'
              '    _titleController = TextEditingController();\n'
              '    _titleFocusNode = FocusNode();\n'
              '    _titleController.addListener(_clearInlineErrors);\n'
              '    if (widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository != null &&\n'
              '        defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.android) {\n'
              '      unawaited(_restoreLostAttachment());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _titleController\n'
              '      ..removeListener(_clearInlineErrors)\n'
              '      ..dispose();\n'
              '    _titleFocusNode.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _clearInlineErrors() {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if ((_titleError != null && _titleController.text.trim().isNotEmpty) ||\n'
              '        _attachmentError != null) {\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        if (_titleController.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '          _titleError = null;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        _attachmentError = null;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _restoreLostAttachment() async {\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final attachment = await recoverLostEvidenceAttachment();\n'
              '      if (attachment != null && mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _selectedAttachment = attachment;\n'
              '          _showMoreOptions = true;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _attachmentError = _describeAttachmentError(error));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _pickAttachment() async {\n'
              '    if (_saving || widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final attachment = await pickEvidenceAttachment(context);\n'
              '      if (attachment == null || !mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _selectedAttachment = attachment;\n'
              '        _attachmentError = null;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _attachmentError = _describeAttachmentError(error));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _describeAttachmentError(Object error) {\n'
              '    return describeEvidenceAttachmentError(error);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _deriveQuickCaptureTitle(String note) {\n'
              '    final normalized = note.replaceAll(RegExp(r\'\\s+\'), \' \').trim();\n'
              '    final punctuationIndex = normalized.indexOf(RegExp(r\'[。！？!?；;]\'));\n'
              '    var candidate = punctuationIndex > 0\n'
              '        ? normalized.substring(0, punctuationIndex)\n'
              '        : normalized;\n'
              '    const maxTitleLength = 48;\n'
              '    if (candidate.length > maxTitleLength) {\n'
              '      candidate = \'${candidate.substring(0, maxTitleLength)}…\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return candidate;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _save() async {\n'
              '    var valid = true;\n'
              '    if (_selectedStudent == null) {\n'
              '      _studentError = \'请选择学生\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final note = _titleController.text.trim();\n'
              '    if (note.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      _titleError = \'请写下今天看到的情况\';\n'
              '      valid = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!valid) {\n'
              '      setState(() {});\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final student = _selectedStudent!;\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _saving = true;\n'
              '      _saveError = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final receipt = await widget.repository.quickCapture(\n'
              '        QuickCaptureCommand(\n'
              '          operationId: _operationId,\n'
              '          profileId: student.profileId,\n'
              '          expectedProfileVersion: student.profileVersion,\n'
              '          caseType: _selectedCaseType.baseType,\n'
              '          organizationCaseTypeId: _selectedCaseType.id,\n'
              '          title: _deriveQuickCaptureTitle(note),\n'
              '          description: note,\n'
              '          observedAt: DateTime.now(),\n'
              '          evidenceSummary: note,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      final attachment = _selectedAttachment;\n'
              '      final attachmentRepository = widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository;\n'
              '      if (attachment != null && attachmentRepository != null) {\n'
              '        final organizationId = widget.organizationId;\n'
              '        if (organizationId == null) {\n'
              '          throw StateError(\'机构信息缺失，无法上传图片。\');\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        try {\n'
              '          await attachmentRepository.upload(\n'
              '            organizationId: organizationId,\n'
              '            learningCaseId: receipt.caseId,\n'
              '            evidenceId: receipt.evidenceId,\n'
              '            attachmentId: attachment.attachmentId,\n'
              '            bytes: attachment.bytes,\n'
              '            fileName: attachment.fileName,\n'
              '            contentType: attachment.contentType,\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        } catch (error) {\n'
              '          if (!mounted) {\n'
              '            return;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          setState(() {\n'
              '            _saving = false;\n'
              '            _saveError =\n'
              '                \'文字已保存，但图片上传失败：\'\n'
              '                \'${describeEvidenceAttachmentError(error, duringUpload: true)} \'\n'
              '                \'请保持当前窗口打开后重试。\';\n'
              '          });\n'
              '          return;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop(true);\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _saving = false;\n'
              '        _saveError = _describeSaveError(error);\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _confirmDiscard() async {\n'
              '    if (!_isDirty && !_saving) {\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop();\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final discard = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '        title: const Text(\'放弃这段记录？\'),\n'
              '        content: const Text(\'当前输入还没有保存。放弃后可以从学生详情重新记录。\'),\n'
              '        actions: [\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'继续编辑\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          FilledButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'放弃记录\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (mounted && discard == true) {\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _describeSaveError(Object error) {\n'
              '    final detail = error.toString().toLowerCase();\n'
              '    if (detail.contains(\'version_conflict\')) {\n'
              '      return \'学生资料已经发生变化。请保留这段输入，返回刷新后再试。\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (detail.contains(\'network\') ||\n'
              '        detail.contains(\'socket\') ||\n'
              '        detail.contains(\'timeout\')) {\n'
              '      return \'网络暂时不可用。输入仍保留在这里，请检查网络后重试。\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return \'保存失败。输入仍保留在这里，请重试；未确认成功前不会生成重复问题。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _selectStudent(WorkspaceStudent? student) {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _selectedStudent = student;\n'
              '      _studentError = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    if (student == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {\n'
              '      if (mounted && !_saving) {\n'
              '        _titleFocusNode.requestFocus();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  bool _isCompact(BuildContext context) =>\n'
              '      ResponsiveBreakpoints.classify(MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width) ==\n'
              '      WindowSizeClass.compact;\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildStudentField(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    if (!_isCompact(context)) {\n'
              '      return DropdownButtonFormField<WorkspaceStudent>(\n'
              '        initialValue: _selectedStudent,\n'
              '        decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '          labelText: \'学生 *\',\n'
              '          errorText: _studentError,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        hint: const Text(\'选择学生后开始\'),\n'
              '        items: [\n'
              '          for (final student in widget.students)\n'
              '            DropdownMenuItem<WorkspaceStudent>(\n'
              '              value: student,\n'
              '              child: Text([student.name, student.subject].join(\' · \')),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        onChanged: _saving ? null : _selectStudent,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final student = _selectedStudent;\n'
              '    return _WorkspaceChoiceField(\n'
              '      fieldKey: const Key(\'quick-capture-student-picker\'),\n'
              '      label: \'学生 *\',\n'
              '      value: student == null\n'
              '          ? \'请选择学生\'\n'
              '          : [student.name, student.subject].join(\' · \'),\n'
              '      errorText: _studentError,\n'
              '      onTap: _saving ? null : _openStudentPicker,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildCaseTypeField(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    if (!_isCompact(context)) {\n'
              '      return DropdownButtonFormField<String>(\n'
              '        key: const Key(\'quick-capture-case-type-dropdown\'),\n'
              '        initialValue: _selectedCaseTypeKey,\n'
              '        decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: \'问题类型（可调整）\'),\n'
              '        items: [\n'
              '          for (final type in _caseTypeOptions)\n'
              '            DropdownMenuItem<String>(value: type.key, child: Text(type.label)),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '        onChanged: _saving\n'
              '            ? null\n'
              '            : (typeKey) {\n'
              '                if (typeKey != null) {\n'
              '                  setState(() => _selectedCaseTypeKey = typeKey);\n'
              '                }\n'
              '              },\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    return _WorkspaceChoiceField(\n'
              '      fieldKey: const Key(\'quick-capture-case-type-dropdown\'),\n'
              '      label: \'问题类型（可调整）\',\n'
              '      value: _selectedCaseType.label,\n'
              '      onTap: _saving ? null : _openCaseTypePicker,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _openStudentPicker() async {\n'
              '    if (_saving || widget.students.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final selectedStudent = await showModalBottomSheet<WorkspaceStudent>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '      useSafeArea: true,\n'
              '      builder: (context) => _WorkspaceChoiceSheet<WorkspaceStudent>(\n'
              '        title: \'选择学生\',\n'
              '        selectedValue: _selectedStudent,\n'
              '        options: [\n'
              '          for (final student in widget.students)\n'
              '            _WorkspaceChoiceOption<WorkspaceStudent>(\n'
              '              key: ValueKey<String>(\n'
              '                \'quick-capture-student-option-${student.profileId}\',\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              value: student,\n'
              '              title: [student.name, student.subject].join(\' · \'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || selectedStudent == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _selectStudent(selectedStudent);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _openCaseTypePicker() async {\n'
              '    if (_saving) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final selectedTypeKey = await showModalBottomSheet<String>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      isScrollControlled: true,\n'
              '      useSafeArea: true,\n'
              '      builder: (context) => _WorkspaceChoiceSheet<String>(\n'
              '        title: \'选择问题类型\',\n'
              '        selectedValue: _selectedCaseTypeKey,\n'
              '        options: [\n'
              '          for (final type in _caseTypeOptions)\n'
              '            _WorkspaceChoiceOption<String>(\n'
              '              key: ValueKey<String>(\n'
              '                \'quick-capture-case-type-option-${type.key}\',\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              value: type.key,\n'
              '              title: type.label,\n'
              '              subtitle: type.isBuiltIn\n'
              '                  ? \'系统类型\'\n'
              '                  : \'自定义类型 · ${type.baseType.label}\',\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || selectedTypeKey == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() => _selectedCaseTypeKey = selectedTypeKey);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final bottomInset = MediaQuery.viewInsetsOf(context).bottom;\n'
              '    final currentCases = _currentCasesForSelectedStudent;\n'
              '    return PopScope<void>(\n'
              '      canPop: !_isDirty && !_saving,\n'
              '      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {\n'
              '        if (!didPop && !_saving) {\n'
              '          _confirmDiscard();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      child: SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: AnimatedPadding(\n'
              '          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),\n'
              '          padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: bottomInset),\n'
              '          child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '            child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
              '              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 560),\n'
              '              child: Column(\n'
              '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                children: [\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: Text(\n'
              '                          \'记录新问题\',\n'
              '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      IconButton(\n'
              '                        tooltip: \'关闭\',\n'
              '                        onPressed: _saving ? null : _confirmDiscard,\n'
              '                        icon: Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Text(\n'
              '                    \'先把刚看到的情况记下来。分类、图片和后续提醒都可以以后再补。\',\n'
              '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(\n'
              '                      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  if (widget.initialStudent != null)\n'
              '                    _WorkspaceContextLine(\n'
              '                      label: \'学生\',\n'
              '                      value: [\n'
              '                        widget.initialStudent!.name,\n'
              '                        widget.initialStudent!.subject,\n'
              '                      ].join(\' · \'),\n'
              '                    )\n'
              '                  else\n'
              '                    _buildStudentField(context),\n'
              '                  if (currentCases.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                    Container(\n'
              '                      key: const Key(\'quick-capture-existing-cases-hint\'),\n'
              '                      width: double.infinity,\n'
              '                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                      decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '                        color: Theme.of(context)\n'
              '                            .colorScheme\n'
              '                            .surfaceContainerHighest\n'
              '                            .withValues(alpha: 0.46),\n'
              '                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadii.compact),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      child: Row(\n'
              '                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                        children: [\n'
              '                          Icon(\n'
              '                            Icons.history_outlined,\n'
              '                            size: 19,\n'
              '                            color: Theme.of(context)\n'
              '                                .colorScheme\n'
              '                                .onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                          const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                          Expanded(\n'
              '                            child: Column(\n'
              '                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                              children: [\n'
              '                                Text(\n'
              '                                  \'这个学生已有正在跟进的问题\',\n'
              '                                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge,\n'
              '                                ),\n'
              '                                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                                Text(\n'
              '                                  [\n'
              '                                    ...currentCases\n'
              '                                        .take(3)\n'
              '                                        .map(\n'
              '                                          (learningCase) => learningCase.title,\n'
              '                                        ),\n'
              '                                    if (currentCases.length > 3)\n'
              '                                      \'另外 ${currentCases.length - 3} 个\',\n'
              '                                  ].join(\' · \'),\n'
              '                                  maxLines: 2,\n'
              '                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
              '                                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                                ),\n'
              '                                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                                Text(\n'
              '                                  \'如果今天看到的是这些问题的新变化，请用“记进展”；确实是新的问题，可以继续记录。\',\n'
              '                                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall\n'
              '                                      ?.copyWith(\n'
              '                                        color: Theme.of(context)\n'
              '                                            .colorScheme\n'
              '                                            .onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '                                      ),\n'
              '                                ),\n'
              '                              ],\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        ],\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  TextField(\n'
              '                    key: const Key(\'quick-capture-title-field\'),\n'
              '                    controller: _titleController,\n'
              '                    focusNode: _titleFocusNode,\n'
              '                    autofocus: _selectedStudent != null,\n'
              '                    enabled: !_saving,\n'
              '                    minLines: 3,\n'
              '                    maxLines: 7,\n'
              '                    textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,\n'
              '                    decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                      labelText: \'今天发现什么？ *\',\n'
              '                      hintText: \'例如：阅读题经常漏看限制词。今天连续两题都没注意“不正确的是”。\',\n'
              '                      errorText: _titleError,\n'
              '                      alignLabelWithHint: true,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Text(\n'
              '                    \'一句话也可以。保存后只进入学生记录，不会自动生成待办。\',\n'
              '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  Align(\n'
              '                    alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,\n'
              '                    child: TextButton.icon(\n'
              '                      key: const Key(\'quick-capture-more-options\'),\n'
              '                      onPressed: _saving\n'
              '                          ? null\n'
              '                          : () => setState(\n'
              '                              () => _showMoreOptions = !_showMoreOptions,\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                      icon: Icon(\n'
              '                        _showMoreOptions\n'
              '                            ? Icons.expand_less\n'
              '                            : Icons.tune_outlined,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      label: Text(_showMoreOptions ? \'收起更多选项\' : \'更多选项\'),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  if (_showMoreOptions) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                    _buildCaseTypeField(context),\n'
              '                    if (widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository != null) ...[\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                      _buildAttachmentField(context),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                  const _WorkspaceContextLine(\n'
              '                    label: \'保存后\',\n'
              '                    value: \'进入学生成长记录，不自动生成待办；需要提醒时再设置。\',\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  if (_saveError != null) ...[\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                    _WorkspaceErrorText(message: _saveError!),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: OutlinedButton(\n'
              '                          onPressed: _saving ? null : _confirmDiscard,\n'
              '                          child: const Text(\'取消\'),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: FilledButton(\n'
              '                          key: const Key(\'workspace-quick-capture-save\'),\n'
              '                          onPressed: _saving ? null : _save,\n'
              '                          child: Text(_saving ? \'保存中…\' : \'记录新问题\'),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildAttachmentField(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final attachment = _selectedAttachment;\n'
              '    return Column(\n'
              '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        Row(\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            Expanded(\n'
              '              child: Text(\n'
              '                \'现场照片（可选）\',\n'
              '                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
              '              onPressed: _saving ? null : _pickAttachment,\n'
              '              icon: const Icon(Icons.add_a_photo_outlined),\n'
              '              label: Text(attachment == null ? \'拍照或选图\' : \'更换图片\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '        if (attachment == null)\n'
              '          Text(\n'
              '            \'图片只用于补充现场信息；主要记录仍以刚才那句话为准。\',\n'
              '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        else\n'
              '          Row(\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              ClipRRect(\n'
              '                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadii.compact),\n'
              '                child: Image.memory(\n'
              '                  attachment.bytes,\n'
              '                  width: 112,\n'
              '                  height: 84,\n'
              '                  fit: BoxFit.cover,\n'
              '                  errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => const SizedBox(\n'
              '                    width: 112,\n'
              '                    height: 84,\n'
              '                    child: Icon(Icons.broken_image_outlined),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '              Expanded(\n'
              '                child: Column(\n'
              '                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      attachment.fileName,\n'
              '                      maxLines: 2,\n'
              '                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      \'${(attachment.bytes.length / 1024 / 1024).toStringAsFixed(1)} MB · 保存后上传\',\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    TextButton.icon(\n'
              '                      onPressed: _saving\n'
              '                          ? null\n'
              '                          : () => setState(() {\n'
              '                              _selectedAttachment = null;\n'
              '                              _attachmentError = null;\n'
              '                            }),\n'
              '                      icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline),\n'
              '                      label: const Text(\'移除\'),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        if (_attachmentError != null) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '          _WorkspaceErrorText(message: _attachmentError!),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceChoiceOption<T> {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceChoiceOption({\n'
              '    required this.key,\n'
              '    required this.value,\n'
              '    required this.title,\n'
              '    this.subtitle,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final Key key;\n'
              '  final T value;\n'
              '  final String title;\n'
              '  final String? subtitle;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceChoiceSheet<T> extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceChoiceSheet({\n'
              '    required this.title,\n'
              '    required this.options,\n'
              '    required this.selectedValue,\n'
              '    super.key,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String title;\n'
              '  final List<_WorkspaceChoiceOption<T>> options;\n'
              '  final T? selectedValue;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
              '    final maxHeight = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height * 0.72;\n'
              '    return SafeArea(\n'
              '      child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
              '        constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: maxHeight),\n'
              '        child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          child: Column(\n'
              '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Padding(\n'
              '                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(\n'
              '                  AppSpacing.lg,\n'
              '                  AppSpacing.md,\n'
              '                  AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '                  AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                child: Row(\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Expanded(\n'
              '                      child: Text(\n'
              '                        title,\n'
              '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    IconButton(\n'
              '                      tooltip: \'关闭\',\n'
              '                      onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),\n'
              '                      icon: Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              for (final option in options)\n'
              '                ListTile(\n'
              '                  key: option.key,\n'
              '                  selected: selectedValue == option.value,\n'
              '                  selectedTileColor: colorScheme.secondaryContainer.withValues(\n'
              '                    alpha: 0.34,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  title: Text(option.title),\n'
              '                  subtitle: option.subtitle == null\n'
              '                      ? null\n'
              '                      : Text(option.subtitle!),\n'
              '                  trailing: selectedValue == option.value\n'
              '                      ? Icon(\n'
              '                          Icons.check,\n'
              '                          color: colorScheme.onSecondaryContainer,\n'
              '                        )\n'
              '                      : null,\n'
              '                  onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(option.value),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceChoiceField extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceChoiceField({\n'
              '    required this.label,\n'
              '    required this.value,\n'
              '    required this.onTap,\n'
              '    this.errorText,\n'
              '    this.fieldKey,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String label;\n'
              '  final String value;\n'
              '  final String? errorText;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onTap;\n'
              '  final Key? fieldKey;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
              '    final valueStyle = Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(\n'
              '      color: onTap == null\n'
              '          ? colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.60)\n'
              '          : colorScheme.onSurface,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return Semantics(\n'
              '      button: true,\n'
              '      enabled: onTap != null,\n'
              '      label: label,\n'
              '      value: value,\n'
              '      child: InkWell(\n'
              '        key: fieldKey,\n'
              '        onTap: onTap,\n'
              '        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadii.medium),\n'
              '        child: InputDecorator(\n'
              '          decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: label, errorText: errorText),\n'
              '          child: Row(\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Expanded(child: Text(value, style: valueStyle)),\n'
              '              Icon(\n'
              '                Icons.keyboard_arrow_down,\n'
              '                color: colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _CaseTypeDraft {\n'
              '  const _CaseTypeDraft({required this.displayName, required this.baseType});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String displayName;\n'
              '  final LearningCaseType baseType;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCaseTypeEditorDialog extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceCaseTypeEditorDialog({\n'
              '    this.initialName = \'\',\n'
              '    this.initialBaseType = LearningCaseType.knowledge,\n'
              '    this.allowBaseTypeChange = true,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String initialName;\n'
              '  final LearningCaseType initialBaseType;\n'
              '  final bool allowBaseTypeChange;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_WorkspaceCaseTypeEditorDialog> createState() =>\n'
              '      _WorkspaceCaseTypeEditorDialogState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCaseTypeEditorDialogState\n'
              '    extends State<_WorkspaceCaseTypeEditorDialog> {\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _nameController;\n'
              '  late LearningCaseType _baseType;\n'
              '  String? _error;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _nameController = TextEditingController(text: widget.initialName);\n'
              '    _baseType = widget.initialBaseType;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _nameController.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _submit() {\n'
              '    final displayName = _nameController.text.trim();\n'
              '    if (displayName.isEmpty) {\n'
              '      setState(() => _error = \'请输入问题类型名称。\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (displayName.length > 64) {\n'
              '      setState(() => _error = \'名称不能超过 64 个字符。\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context)\n'
              '        .pop(_CaseTypeDraft(displayName: displayName, baseType: _baseType));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return AlertDialog(\n'
              '      title: Text(widget.initialName.isEmpty ? \'新增问题类型\' : \'重命名问题类型\'),\n'
              '      content: ConstrainedBox(\n'
              '        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 420),\n'
              '        child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '          child: Column(\n'
              '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              TextField(\n'
              '                controller: _nameController,\n'
              '                autofocus: true,\n'
              '                maxLength: 64,\n'
              '                decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
              '                  labelText: \'显示名称\',\n'
              '                  hintText: \'例如：审题习惯、计算步骤\',\n'
              '                  errorText: _error,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                onSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '              if (widget.allowBaseTypeChange)\n'
              '                DropdownButtonFormField<LearningCaseType>(\n'
              '                  initialValue: _baseType,\n'
              '                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: \'归入基础分类\'),\n'
              '                  items: [\n'
              '                    for (final type in LearningCaseType.values)\n'
              '                      DropdownMenuItem<LearningCaseType>(\n'
              '                        value: type,\n'
              '                        child: Text(type.label),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                  onChanged: (type) {\n'
              '                    if (type != null) {\n'
              '                      setState(() => _baseType = type);\n'
              '                    }\n'
              '                  },\n'
              '                )\n'
              '              else\n'
              '                _WorkspaceContextLine(label: \'基础分类\', value: _baseType.label),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '      actions: [\n'
              '        TextButton(\n'
              '          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),\n'
              '          child: const Text(\'取消\'),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        FilledButton(onPressed: _submit, child: const Text(\'保存\')),\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCaseTypeManager extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceCaseTypeManager({\n'
              '    required this.organizationId,\n'
              '    required this.caseTypes,\n'
              '    required this.repository,\n'
              '    this.onChanged,\n'
              '    this.showCloseButton = true,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String organizationId;\n'
              '  final List<WorkspaceCaseType> caseTypes;\n'
              '  final LearningRepository repository;\n'
              '  final Future<void> Function()? onChanged;\n'
              '  final bool showCloseButton;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_WorkspaceCaseTypeManager> createState() =>\n'
              '      _WorkspaceCaseTypeManagerState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCaseTypeManagerState extends State<_WorkspaceCaseTypeManager> {\n'
              '  late List<WorkspaceCaseType> _caseTypes;\n'
              '  bool _busy = false;\n'
              '  String? _error;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _caseTypes = List<WorkspaceCaseType>.of(widget.caseTypes);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _createCaseType() async {\n'
              '    final draft = await showDialog<_CaseTypeDraft>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (_) => const _WorkspaceCaseTypeEditorDialog(),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || draft == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await _runMutation(\n'
              '      () => widget.repository.createCaseType(\n'
              '        organizationId: widget.organizationId,\n'
              '        displayName: draft.displayName,\n'
              '        baseType: draft.baseType,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _renameCaseType(WorkspaceCaseType caseType) async {\n'
              '    final caseTypeId = caseType.id;\n'
              '    if (caseTypeId == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final draft = await showDialog<_CaseTypeDraft>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (_) => _WorkspaceCaseTypeEditorDialog(\n'
              '        initialName: caseType.label,\n'
              '        initialBaseType: caseType.baseType,\n'
              '        allowBaseTypeChange: false,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || draft == null) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await _runMutation(\n'
              '      () => widget.repository.renameCaseType(\n'
              '        caseTypeId: caseTypeId,\n'
              '        displayName: draft.displayName,\n'
              '        expectedVersion: caseType.version,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _archiveCaseType(WorkspaceCaseType caseType) async {\n'
              '    final caseTypeId = caseType.id;\n'
              '    if (caseTypeId == null || !caseType.isActive) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final shouldArchive = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '        title: const Text(\'归档这个问题类型？\'),\n'
              '        content: Text(\'归档后不能用于新记录，但已有“${caseType.label}”的问题历史仍会保留。\'),\n'
              '        actions: [\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'取消\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          FilledButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),\n'
              '            child: const Text(\'归档\'),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || shouldArchive != true) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await _runMutation(\n'
              '      () => widget.repository.archiveCaseType(\n'
              '        caseTypeId: caseTypeId,\n'
              '        expectedVersion: caseType.version,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _runMutation(\n'
              '    Future<WorkspaceCaseType> Function() mutation,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _busy = true;\n'
              '      _error = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final updated = await mutation();\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        final index = _caseTypes.indexWhere((item) => item.key == updated.key);\n'
              '        if (index == -1) {\n'
              '          _caseTypes = [..._caseTypes, updated];\n'
              '        } else {\n'
              '          _caseTypes = [\n'
              '            ..._caseTypes.sublist(0, index),\n'
              '            updated,\n'
              '            ..._caseTypes.sublist(index + 1),\n'
              '          ];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      final onChanged = widget.onChanged;\n'
              '      if (onChanged != null) {\n'
              '        await onChanged();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _error = _describeCaseTypeError(error));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _busy = false);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _describeCaseTypeError(Object error) {\n'
              '    final detail = error.toString().toLowerCase();\n'
              '    if (detail.contains(\'case_type_name_taken\')) {\n'
              '      return \'这个名称已经存在，请换一个名称。\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (detail.contains(\'invalid_case_type_input\')) {\n'
              '      return \'名称不能与系统类型重复，且不能超过 64 个字符。\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (detail.contains(\'version_conflict\')) {\n'
              '      return \'类型列表已经更新，请关闭后重新打开再操作。\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (detail.contains(\'case_type_archived\')) {\n'
              '      return \'这个类型已经归档，请刷新列表。\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (detail.contains(\'case_type_manager_required\') ||\n'
              '        detail.contains(\'permission\')) {\n'
              '      return \'当前账号没有修改机构问题类型的权限。\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (detail.contains(\'network\') ||\n'
              '        detail.contains(\'socket\') ||\n'
              '        detail.contains(\'timeout\')) {\n'
              '      return \'网络暂时不可用，列表和输入都保留，请稍后重试。\';\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return \'操作失败，列表没有被静默改写，请重试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  List<WorkspaceCaseType> _customTypes({required bool active}) {\n'
              '    final result = _caseTypes\n'
              '        .where((caseType) => !caseType.isBuiltIn && caseType.isActive == active)\n'
              '        .toList();\n'
              '    result.sort((left, right) {\n'
              '      final order = left.sortOrder.compareTo(right.sortOrder);\n'
              '      if (order != 0) {\n'
              '        return order;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return left.label.compareTo(right.label);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    return result;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _typeRow(WorkspaceCaseType caseType) {\n'
              '    return Container(\n'
              '      width: double.infinity,\n'
              '      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(\n'
              '        horizontal: AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '        vertical: AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '      decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surfaceContainerHigh,\n'
              '        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadii.small),\n'
              '        border: Border.all(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outlineVariant),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '      child: Row(\n'
              '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          Icon(\n'
              '            Icons.label_outline,\n'
              '            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          Expanded(\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                Text(caseType.label),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                Text(\n'
              '                  \'基础分类：${caseType.baseType.label}\',\n'
              '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          if (caseType.isActive)\n'
              '            PopupMenuButton<String>(\n'
              '              enabled: !_busy,\n'
              '              tooltip: \'更多操作\',\n'
              '              onSelected: (value) {\n'
              '                if (value == \'rename\') {\n'
              '                  _renameCaseType(caseType);\n'
              '                } else if (value == \'archive\') {\n'
              '                  _archiveCaseType(caseType);\n'
              '                }\n'
              '              },\n'
              '              itemBuilder: (context) => const [\n'
              '                PopupMenuItem<String>(value: \'rename\', child: Text(\'重命名\')),\n'
              '                PopupMenuItem<String>(value: \'archive\', child: Text(\'归档\')),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            )\n'
              '          else\n'
              '            Padding(\n'
              '              padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '              child: Text(\'已归档\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _typeSection({\n'
              '    required String title,\n'
              '    required List<WorkspaceCaseType> types,\n'
              '    required String emptyTitle,\n'
              '    required String emptyMessage,\n'
              '    required IconData icon,\n'
              '  }) {\n'
              '    return _WorkspaceSection(\n'
              '      title: title,\n'
              '      count: \'${types.length} 个\',\n'
              '      child: types.isEmpty\n'
              '          ? _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '              title: emptyTitle,\n'
              '              message: emptyMessage,\n'
              '              icon: icon,\n'
              '            )\n'
              '          : Column(children: [for (final type in types) _typeRow(type)]),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final bottomInset = MediaQuery.viewInsetsOf(context).bottom;\n'
              '    final activeTypes = _customTypes(active: true);\n'
              '    final archivedTypes = _customTypes(active: false);\n'
              '    return SafeArea(\n'
              '      child: AnimatedPadding(\n'
              '        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),\n'
              '        padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: bottomInset),\n'
              '        child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '          child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
              '            constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 560),\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                Row(\n'
              '                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Expanded(\n'
              '                      child: Text(\n'
              '                        \'问题类型\',\n'
              '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    if (widget.showCloseButton)\n'
              '                      IconButton(\n'
              '                        tooltip: \'关闭\',\n'
              '                        onPressed: _busy\n'
              '                            ? null\n'
              '                            : () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),\n'
              '                        icon: Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                Text(\n'
              '                  \'系统类型始终保留。自定义类型只负责分类，所有问题仍沿用同一套证据、行动和验证流程。\',\n'
              '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(\n'
              '                    color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                if (_error != null) ...[\n'
              '                  _WorkspaceErrorText(message: _error!),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '                _typeSection(\n'
              '                  title: \'可用于新记录\',\n'
              '                  types: activeTypes,\n'
              '                  emptyTitle: \'还没有自定义类型\',\n'
              '                  emptyMessage: \'先添加一个贴合你们教学语言的分类，教师记录问题时就能直接选择。\',\n'
              '                  icon: Icons.category_outlined,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                _typeSection(\n'
              '                  title: \'已归档\',\n'
              '                  types: archivedTypes,\n'
              '                  emptyTitle: \'没有已归档类型\',\n'
              '                  emptyMessage: \'归档后仍会保留历史名称，不会改变已有问题。\',\n'
              '                  icon: Icons.archive_outlined,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                SizedBox(\n'
              '                  width: double.infinity,\n'
              '                  child: FilledButton.icon(\n'
              '                    onPressed: _busy ? null : _createCaseType,\n'
              '                    icon: Icon(Icons.add),\n'
              '                    label: const Text(\'新增自定义类型\'),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCaseCommandSection extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceCaseCommandSection({\n'
              '    required this.title,\n'
              '    required this.message,\n'
              '    required this.buttonLabel,\n'
              '    required this.onPressed,\n'
              '    this.secondaryButtonLabel,\n'
              '    this.onSecondaryPressed,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String title;\n'
              '  final String message;\n'
              '  final String buttonLabel;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onPressed;\n'
              '  final String? secondaryButtonLabel;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onSecondaryPressed;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Container(\n'
              '      width: double.infinity,\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '      decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surfaceContainerHigh,\n'
              '        border: Border.all(\n'
              '          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.35),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadii.medium),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '      child: Row(\n'
              '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          Icon(\n'
              '            Icons.arrow_circle_right_outlined,\n'
              '            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          Expanded(\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                Text(message, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                FilledButton.icon(\n'
              '                  onPressed: onPressed,\n'
              '                  icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_forward, size: 18),\n'
              '                  label: Text(buttonLabel),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                if (secondaryButtonLabel != null &&\n'
              '                    onSecondaryPressed != null) ...[\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                  OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
              '                    onPressed: onSecondaryPressed,\n'
