              '                    icon: const Icon(Icons.stop_circle_outlined, size: 18),\n'
              '                    label: Text(secondaryButtonLabel!),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceShell extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceShell({\n'
              '    required this.selectedIndex,\n'
              '    required this.onDestinationSelected,\n'
              '    required this.hasTeachingAccess,\n'
              '    required this.showManagement,\n'
              '    required this.child,\n'
              '    this.onSignOut,\n'
              '    this.onCheckForUpdates,\n'
              '    this.checkingForUpdates = false,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final int selectedIndex;\n'
              '  final ValueChanged<int> onDestinationSelected;\n'
              '  final bool hasTeachingAccess;\n'
              '  final bool showManagement;\n'
              '  final Widget child;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onSignOut;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onCheckForUpdates;\n'
              '  final bool checkingForUpdates;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    // A management-only account has one valid workspace surface. Flutter\'s\n'
              '    // NavigationBar/NavigationRail require at least two destinations, so do\n'
              '    // not render a navigation shell that would manufacture a second tab or\n'
              '    // expose a teaching page outside the account\'s scope.\n'
              '    if (!hasTeachingAccess && showManagement) {\n'
              '      return Scaffold(\n'
              '        appBar: AppBar(\n'
              '          title: const Text(\'学情闭环\'),\n'
              '          actions: [\n'
              '            if (onCheckForUpdates != null)\n'
              '              IconButton(\n'
              '                tooltip: \'检查更新\',\n'
              '                onPressed: checkingForUpdates ? null : onCheckForUpdates,\n'
              '                icon: checkingForUpdates\n'
              '                    ? const SizedBox(\n'
              '                        width: 20,\n'
              '                        height: 20,\n'
              '                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),\n'
              '                      )\n'
              '                    : const Icon(Icons.system_update_alt),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            if (onSignOut != null)\n'
              '              IconButton(\n'
              '                tooltip: \'退出登录\',\n'
              '                onPressed: onSignOut,\n'
              '                icon: const Icon(Icons.logout),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        body: SafeArea(child: child),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    return ResponsiveLayout(\n'
              '      builder: (context, sizeClass) {\n'
              '        if (sizeClass == WindowSizeClass.compact) {\n'
              '          return Scaffold(\n'
              '            appBar: AppBar(\n'
              '              title: const Text(\'学情闭环\'),\n'
              '              actions: [\n'
              '                if (onCheckForUpdates != null)\n'
              '                  IconButton(\n'
              '                    tooltip: \'检查更新\',\n'
              '                    onPressed: checkingForUpdates ? null : onCheckForUpdates,\n'
              '                    icon: checkingForUpdates\n'
              '                        ? const SizedBox(\n'
              '                            width: 20,\n'
              '                            height: 20,\n'
              '                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),\n'
              '                          )\n'
              '                        : const Icon(Icons.system_update_alt),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                if (onSignOut != null)\n'
              '                  IconButton(\n'
              '                    tooltip: \'退出登录\',\n'
              '                    onPressed: onSignOut,\n'
              '                    icon: Icon(Icons.logout),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            body: SafeArea(child: child),\n'
              '            bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(\n'
              '              selectedIndex: selectedIndex,\n'
              '              onDestinationSelected: onDestinationSelected,\n'
              '              destinations: _workspaceDestinations(\n'
              '                hasTeachingAccess: hasTeachingAccess,\n'
              '                showManagement: showManagement,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        return Scaffold(\n'
              '          body: SafeArea(\n'
              '            child: Row(\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                _WorkspaceRail(\n'
              '                  extended: sizeClass == WindowSizeClass.expanded,\n'
              '                  selectedIndex: selectedIndex,\n'
              '                  onDestinationSelected: onDestinationSelected,\n'
              '                  hasTeachingAccess: hasTeachingAccess,\n'
              '                  showManagement: showManagement,\n'
              '                  onSignOut: onSignOut,\n'
              '                  onCheckForUpdates: onCheckForUpdates,\n'
              '                  checkingForUpdates: checkingForUpdates,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                const VerticalDivider(width: 1),\n'
              '                Expanded(child: child),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceRail extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceRail({\n'
              '    required this.extended,\n'
              '    required this.selectedIndex,\n'
              '    required this.onDestinationSelected,\n'
              '    required this.hasTeachingAccess,\n'
              '    required this.showManagement,\n'
              '    this.onSignOut,\n'
              '    this.onCheckForUpdates,\n'
              '    this.checkingForUpdates = false,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final bool extended;\n'
              '  final int selectedIndex;\n'
              '  final ValueChanged<int> onDestinationSelected;\n'
              '  final bool hasTeachingAccess;\n'
              '  final bool showManagement;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onSignOut;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onCheckForUpdates;\n'
              '  final bool checkingForUpdates;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return SizedBox(\n'
              '      width: extended ? 232 : 80,\n'
              '      child: Column(\n'
              '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          Padding(\n'
              '            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(\n'
              '              extended ? AppSpacing.lg : AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '              AppSpacing.lg,\n'
              '              AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '              AppSpacing.md,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            child: extended\n'
              '                ? Column(\n'
              '                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Text(\n'
              '                        \'学情闭环\',\n'
              '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                      Text(\n'
              '                        hasTeachingAccess ? \'教师工作台\' : \'机构管理\',\n'
              '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  )\n'
              '                : const Center(child: Icon(Icons.menu_book_outlined, size: 24)),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const Divider(height: 1),\n'
              '          Expanded(\n'
              '            child: NavigationRail(\n'
              '              extended: extended,\n'
              '              minExtendedWidth: 232,\n'
              '              backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,\n'
              '              selectedIndex: selectedIndex,\n'
              '              onDestinationSelected: onDestinationSelected,\n'
              '              labelType: extended\n'
              '                  ? NavigationRailLabelType.none\n'
              '                  : NavigationRailLabelType.all,\n'
              '              indicatorColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primaryContainer,\n'
              '              selectedIconTheme: IconThemeData(\n'
              '                color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              unselectedIconTheme: IconThemeData(\n'
              '                color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              destinations: _workspaceRailDestinations(\n'
              '                hasTeachingAccess: hasTeachingAccess,\n'
              '                showManagement: showManagement,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          Padding(\n'
              '            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(\n'
              '              extended ? AppSpacing.lg : AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '              AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '              AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '              AppSpacing.lg,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            child: extended\n'
              '                ? Row(\n'
              '                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      if (onCheckForUpdates != null)\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          key: const Key(\'workspace-rail-update\'),\n'
              '                          tooltip: \'检查更新\',\n'
              '                          onPressed: checkingForUpdates\n'
              '                              ? null\n'
              '                              : onCheckForUpdates,\n'
              '                          icon: checkingForUpdates\n'
              '                              ? const SizedBox(\n'
              '                                  width: 20,\n'
              '                                  height: 20,\n'
              '                                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(\n'
              '                                    strokeWidth: 2,\n'
              '                                  ),\n'
              '                                )\n'
              '                              : const Icon(Icons.system_update_alt),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      if (onSignOut != null)\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          key: const Key(\'workspace-rail-sign-out\'),\n'
              '                          tooltip: \'退出登录\',\n'
              '                          onPressed: onSignOut,\n'
              '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.logout),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  )\n'
              '                : Column(\n'
              '                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      if (onCheckForUpdates != null)\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          key: const Key(\'workspace-rail-update\'),\n'
              '                          tooltip: \'检查更新\',\n'
              '                          onPressed: checkingForUpdates\n'
              '                              ? null\n'
              '                              : onCheckForUpdates,\n'
              '                          icon: checkingForUpdates\n'
              '                              ? const SizedBox(\n'
              '                                  width: 20,\n'
              '                                  height: 20,\n'
              '                                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(\n'
              '                                    strokeWidth: 2,\n'
              '                                  ),\n'
              '                                )\n'
              '                              : const Icon(Icons.system_update_alt),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      if (onSignOut != null)\n'
              '                        IconButton(\n'
              '                          key: const Key(\'workspace-rail-sign-out\'),\n'
              '                          tooltip: \'退出登录\',\n'
              '                          onPressed: onSignOut,\n'
              '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.logout),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'List<List<WorkspaceStudent>> _groupStudentsById(\n'
              '  Iterable<WorkspaceStudent> students,\n'
              ') {\n'
              '  final groups = <String, List<WorkspaceStudent>>{};\n'
              '  for (final student in students) {\n'
              '    groups.putIfAbsent(student.id, () => <WorkspaceStudent>[]).add(student);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return groups.values.toList(growable: false);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'List<WorkspaceStudent> _dedupeStudentsById(\n'
              '  Iterable<WorkspaceStudent> students,\n'
              ') {\n'
              '  final seen = <String>{};\n'
              '  return <WorkspaceStudent>[\n'
              '    for (final student in students)\n'
              '      if (seen.add(student.id)) student,\n'
              '  ];\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'List<NavigationDestination> _workspaceDestinations({\n'
              '  required bool hasTeachingAccess,\n'
              '  required bool showManagement,\n'
              '}) {\n'
              '  return [\n'
              '    if (hasTeachingAccess)\n'
              '      const NavigationDestination(\n'
              '        icon: Icon(Icons.today_outlined),\n'
              '        selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.today),\n'
              '        label: \'今日\',\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    if (hasTeachingAccess)\n'
              '      const NavigationDestination(\n'
              '        icon: Icon(Icons.people_outline),\n'
              '        selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.people),\n'
              '        label: \'学生\',\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    if (showManagement)\n'
              '      const NavigationDestination(\n'
              '        icon: Icon(Icons.admin_panel_settings_outlined),\n'
              '        selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.admin_panel_settings),\n'
              '        label: \'管理\',\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '  ];\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'List<NavigationRailDestination> _workspaceRailDestinations({\n'
              '  required bool hasTeachingAccess,\n'
              '  required bool showManagement,\n'
              '}) {\n'
              '  return [\n'
              '    if (hasTeachingAccess)\n'
              '      const NavigationRailDestination(\n'
              '        icon: Icon(Icons.today_outlined),\n'
              '        selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.today),\n'
              '        label: Text(\'今日\'),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    if (hasTeachingAccess)\n'
              '      const NavigationRailDestination(\n'
              '        icon: Icon(Icons.people_outline),\n'
              '        selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.people),\n'
              '        label: Text(\'学生\'),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    if (showManagement)\n'
              '      const NavigationRailDestination(\n'
              '        icon: Icon(Icons.admin_panel_settings_outlined),\n'
              '        selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.admin_panel_settings),\n'
              '        label: Text(\'管理\'),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '  ];\n'
              '}\n'

              'class _WorkspacePageHeader extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspacePageHeader({\n'
              '    required this.title,\n'
              '    this.subtitle,\n'
              '    this.leading,\n'
              '    this.actions = const <Widget>[],\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String title;\n'
              '  final String? subtitle;\n'
              '  final Widget? leading;\n'
              '  final List<Widget> actions;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final titleBlock = Column(\n'
              '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall),\n'
              '        if (subtitle != null) ...[\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '          Text(\n'
              '            subtitle!,\n'
              '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(\n'
              '              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return Padding(\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '      child: LayoutBuilder(\n'
              '        builder: (context, constraints) {\n'
              '          final canFitActions = constraints.maxWidth >= 560;\n'
              '          final leadingBlock = Row(\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              if (leading != null) ...[\n'
              '                leading!,\n'
              '                const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '              Expanded(child: titleBlock),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          );\n'
              '          if (!canFitActions && actions.isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '            return Column(\n'
              '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                leadingBlock,\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                Wrap(\n'
              '                  spacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '                  runSpacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '                  children: actions,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            );\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          return Row(\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Expanded(child: leadingBlock),\n'
              '              if (actions.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '                const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                Flexible(\n'
              '                  child: Wrap(\n'
              '                    alignment: WrapAlignment.end,\n'
              '                    spacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '                    runSpacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '                    children: actions,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        },\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'

              'class _WorkspaceSection extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceSection({\n'
              '    required this.title,\n'
              '    required this.child,\n'
              '    this.count,\n'
              '    this.action,\n'
              '    this.showTopDivider = false,\n'
              '    super.key,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String title;\n'
              '  final Widget child;\n'
              '  final String? count;\n'
              '  final Widget? action;\n'
              '  final bool showTopDivider;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Column(\n'
              '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        if (showTopDivider) const Divider(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '        Padding(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          child: Row(\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Expanded(\n'
              '                child: Row(\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium),\n'
              '                    if (count != null) ...[\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                      Text(\n'
              '                        count!,\n'
              '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              ?action,\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        child,\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'

              'class _WorkspaceStudentRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceStudentRow({required this.student, required this.onOpen});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceStudent student;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onOpen;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final activeCaseCount = student.cases\n'
              '        .where(\n'
              '          (learningCase) => learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.closed,\n'
              '        )\n'
              '        .length;\n'
              '    return Semantics(\n'
              '      button: true,\n'
              '      label: \'打开 ${student.name} 的学生详情\',\n'
              '      child: InkWell(\n'
              '        onTap: onOpen,\n'
              '        child: Padding(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '          child: Row(\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Padding(\n'
              '                padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                child: Icon(Icons.person_outline, size: 21),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '              Expanded(\n'
              '                child: Column(\n'
              '                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      student.name,\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                    Wrap(\n'
              '                      spacing: AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '                      runSpacing: AppSpacing.xxs,\n'
              '                      children: [\n'
              '                        _WorkspaceMetadata(\n'
              '                          \'${student.grade} · ${student.subject}\',\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                        _WorkspaceMetadata(student.context),\n'
              '                      ],\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      activeCaseCount == 0\n'
              '                          ? \'暂无需要跟进的问题\'\n'
              '                          : \'$activeCaseCount 个跟进中的问题\',\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '              Icon(\n'
              '                Icons.chevron_right,\n'
              '                color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceMultiSubjectStudentRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceMultiSubjectStudentRow({\n'
              '    required this.students,\n'
              '    required this.onOpen,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final List<WorkspaceStudent> students;\n'
              '  final ValueChanged<WorkspaceStudent> onOpen;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final profiles = List<WorkspaceStudent>.of(students)\n'
              '      ..sort((left, right) => left.subject.compareTo(right.subject));\n'
              '    final student = profiles.first;\n'
              '    return Padding(\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '      child: Row(\n'
              '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          const Padding(\n'
              '            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '            child: Icon(Icons.person_outline, size: 21),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          Expanded(\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                Text(\n'
              '                  student.name,\n'
              '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                _WorkspaceMetadata(student.grade),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                for (var index = 0; index < profiles.length; index++) ...[\n'
              '                  if (index > 0) const Divider(height: 1),\n'
              '                  _WorkspaceStudentSubjectLink(\n'
              '                    student: profiles[index],\n'
              '                    onOpen: () => onOpen(profiles[index]),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceStudentSubjectLink extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceStudentSubjectLink({\n'
              '    required this.student,\n'
              '    required this.onOpen,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceStudent student;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onOpen;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final activeCaseCount = student.cases\n'
              '        .where(\n'
              '          (learningCase) => learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.closed,\n'
              '        )\n'
              '        .length;\n'
              '    final followUpLabel = activeCaseCount == 0\n'
              '        ? \'暂无需要跟进的问题\'\n'
              '        : \'$activeCaseCount 个跟进中的问题\';\n'
              '    return Semantics(\n'
              '      button: true,\n'
              '      label: \'打开 ${student.name} 的 ${student.subject} 学情\',\n'
              '      child: InkWell(\n'
              '        key: ValueKey<String>(\'workspace-student-subject-${student.profileId}\'),\n'
              '        onTap: onOpen,\n'
              '        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadii.small),\n'
              '        child: Padding(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          child: Row(\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Expanded(\n'
              '                child: Column(\n'
              '                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      student.subject,\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    if (student.context.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                      Text(\n'
              '                        student.context,\n'
              '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(\n'
              '                          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      followUpLabel,\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '              Icon(\n'
              '                Icons.chevron_right,\n'
              '                color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCaseRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceCaseRow({\n'
              '    required this.student,\n'
              '    required this.learningCase,\n'
              '    required this.onOpen,\n'
              '    this.onRecordProgress,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceStudent student;\n'
              '  final WorkspaceCase learningCase;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onOpen;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onRecordProgress;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final primaryAction = learningCase.primaryAction;\n'
              '    return Container(\n'
              '      decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '        border: Border(\n'
              '          bottom: BorderSide(\n'
              '            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outlineVariant,\n'
              '          ),\n'

              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.md),\n'

              '          Row(\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Expanded(\n'
              '                child: Text(\n'
              '                  learningCase.title,\n'
              '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '              _WorkspaceStatusMarker(label: learningCase.status.label),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '          Wrap(\n'
              '            spacing: AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '            runSpacing: AppSpacing.xxs,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              _WorkspaceMetadata(\'${student.name} · ${student.subject}\'),\n'
              '              _WorkspaceMetadata(_priorityLabel(learningCase.priority)),\n'
              '              if (primaryAction != null)\n'
              '                _WorkspaceMetadata(\'下一步：${primaryAction.title}\'),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          Wrap(\n'
              '            spacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '            runSpacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              if (onRecordProgress != null)\n'
              '                FilledButton.tonalIcon(\n'
              '                  key: ValueKey<String>(\'case-row-progress-${learningCase.id}\'),\n'
              '                  onPressed: onRecordProgress,\n'
              '                  icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_note_outlined, size: 18),\n'
              '                  label: const Text(\'记进展\'),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              OutlinedButton(onPressed: onOpen, child: const Text(\'查看问题\')),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceActionGroup extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceActionGroup({\n'
              '    required this.student,\n'
              '    required this.items,\n'
              '    required this.progressiveFlow,\n'
              '    required this.onOpenCase,\n'
              '    required this.onReschedule,\n'
              '    required this.onComplete,\n'
              '    this.reschedulingActionId,\n'
              '    this.completingActionId,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceStudent student;\n'
              '  final List<WorkspaceActionWithContext> items;\n'
              '  final bool progressiveFlow;\n'
              '  final ValueChanged<WorkspaceCase> onOpenCase;\n'
              '  final Future<void> Function(WorkspaceActionWithContext item) onReschedule;\n'
              '  final Future<void> Function(WorkspaceActionWithContext item) onComplete;\n'
              '  final String? reschedulingActionId;\n'
              '  final String? completingActionId;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Padding(\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '      child: Column(\n'
              '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          Padding(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '            child: Text(\n'
              '              \'${student.name} · ${student.subject}\',\n'
              '              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          for (final item in items)\n'
              '            Padding(\n'
              '              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '              child: Column(\n'
              '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                children: [\n'
              '                  Row(\n'
              '                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                    children: [\n'
              '                      Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_outlined, size: 21),\n'
              '                      const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                      Expanded(\n'
              '                        child: Column(\n'
              '                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                          children: [\n'
              '                            Text(\n'
              '                              item.action.title,\n'
              '                              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall,\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                            Wrap(\n'
              '                              spacing: AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '                              runSpacing: AppSpacing.xxs,\n'
              '                              children: [\n'
              '                                _WorkspaceMetadata(item.learningCase.title),\n'
              '                                _WorkspaceMetadata(\n'
              '                                  _formatActionDate(item.action),\n'
              '                                  icon: Icons.event_outlined,\n'
              '                                ),\n'
              '                              ],\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                          ],\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                  Padding(\n'
              '                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 33),\n'
              '                    child: Wrap(\n'
              '                      spacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '                      runSpacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '                      children: [\n'
              '                        if (item.learningCase.status !=\n'
              '                            LearningCaseStatus.newCase)\n'
              '                          FilledButton.icon(\n'
              '                            onPressed: completingActionId == item.action.id\n'
              '                                ? null\n'
              '                                : () => onComplete(item),\n'
              '                            icon: Icon(\n'
              '                              progressiveFlow\n'
              '                                  ? Icons.check_circle_outline\n'
              '                                  : Icons.check,\n'
              '                            ),\n'
              '                            label: Text(progressiveFlow ? \'处理\' : \'完成行动\'),\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        OutlinedButton(\n'
              '                          onPressed: () => onOpenCase(item.learningCase),\n'
              '                          child: const Text(\'查看问题\'),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                        TextButton.icon(\n'
              '                          onPressed: reschedulingActionId == item.action.id\n'
              '                              ? null\n'
              '                              : () => onReschedule(item),\n'
              '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.event_repeat_outlined),\n'
              '                          label: Text(\n'
              '                            item.action.dueAt == null ? \'安排日期\' : \'改期\',\n'
              '                          ),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ],\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          const Divider(height: 1),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCompleteActionForm extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceCompleteActionForm({\n'
              '    required this.action,\n'
              '    required this.learningCase,\n'
              '    required this.repository,\n'
              '    required this.businessDate,\n'
              '    this.progressiveRepository,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceAction action;\n'
              '  final WorkspaceCase learningCase;\n'
              '  final LearningRepository repository;\n'
              '  final ProgressiveCaseRepository? progressiveRepository;\n'
              '  final DateTime? businessDate;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_WorkspaceCompleteActionForm> createState() =>\n'
              '      _WorkspaceCompleteActionFormState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceCompleteActionFormState\n'
              '    extends State<_WorkspaceCompleteActionForm> {\n'
              '  late final String _operationId;\n'
              '  late final TextEditingController _progressController;\n'
              '  CompleteCaseActionCommand? _submittedCommand;\n'
              '  RecordCaseProgressCommand? _submittedProgressCommand;\n'
              '  String? _saveError;\n'
              '  bool _saving = false;\n'
              '  bool _submissionAttempted = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  bool get _inputsLocked => _saving || _submissionAttempted;\n'
              '  bool get _isDirty => _progressController.text.trim().isNotEmpty;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _operationId = createOperationId();\n'
              '    _progressController = TextEditingController();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _progressController.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _save() async {\n'
              '    if (_saving) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final summary = _progressController.text.trim();\n'
              '    final progressiveRepository = widget.progressiveRepository;\n'
              '    if (_submittedCommand == null &&\n'
              '        _submittedProgressCommand == null &&\n'
              '        summary.isNotEmpty &&\n'
              '        progressiveRepository != null) {\n'
              '      _submittedProgressCommand = RecordCaseProgressCommand(\n'
              '        operationId: _operationId,\n'
              '        caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '        expectedCaseVersion: widget.learningCase.version,\n'
              '        progressKind: CaseProgressKind.observation,\n'
              '        summary: summary,\n'
              '        completeCurrentAction: true,\n'
              '        currentActionId: widget.action.id,\n'
              '        expectedActionVersion: widget.action.version,\n'
              '        nextStep: CaseProgressNextStep.continueTracking,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _submittedCommand ??= _submittedProgressCommand == null\n'
              '        ? CompleteCaseActionCommand(\n'
              '            operationId: _operationId,\n'
              '            actionId: widget.action.id,\n'
              '            caseId: widget.learningCase.id,\n'
              '            expectedCaseVersion: widget.learningCase.version,\n'
              '            expectedActionVersion: widget.action.version,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        : null;\n'
              '\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _saving = true;\n'
              '      _submissionAttempted = true;\n'
              '      _saveError = null;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final progressCommand = _submittedProgressCommand;\n'
              '      if (progressCommand != null) {\n'
              '        await progressiveRepository!.recordProgress(progressCommand);\n'
              '        if (!mounted) {\n'
              '          return;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        Navigator.of(context).pop(true);\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      await widget.repository.completeCaseAction(_submittedCommand!);\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop(false);\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _saving = false;\n'
              '        _saveError =\n'
              '            \'${_describeCaseCommandError(error)}\\n刚才填写的内容已保留；可以直接重试，不会重复保存。\';\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _close() async {\n'
              '    if (_saving) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_submissionAttempted) {\n'
              '      final retry = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        builder: (context) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '          title: const Text(\'刚才是否保存成功还不能确认\'),\n'
              '          content: const Text(\'刚才的记录可能已经保存成功。请点“重新保存”继续，系统不会重复保存。\'),\n'
              '          actions: [\n'
              '            TextButton(\n'
              '              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),\n'
              '              child: const Text(\'继续查看\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            FilledButton(\n'
              '              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),\n'
              '              child: const Text(\'重新保存\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (mounted && retry == true) {\n'
              '        await _save();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_isDirty) {\n'
              '      final discard = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        builder: (context) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '          title: const Text(\'放弃这次处理？\'),\n'
              '          content: const Text(\'刚才填写的新情况还没有保存。\'),\n'
              '          actions: [\n'
              '            TextButton(\n'
              '              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),\n'
              '              child: const Text(\'继续编辑\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            FilledButton(\n'
              '              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),\n'
              '              child: const Text(\'放弃\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (!mounted || discard != true) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context).pop();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return PopScope<void>(\n'
              '      canPop: !_isDirty && !_saving && !_submissionAttempted,\n'
              '      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {\n'
              '        if (!didPop && !_saving) {\n'
              '          unawaited(_close());\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '        child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
              '          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 520),\n'
              '          child: Column(\n'
              '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Row(\n'
              '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                children: [\n'
              '                  Expanded(\n'
              '                    child: Text(\n'
              '                      \'处理提醒\',\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  IconButton(\n'
              '                    tooltip: \'关闭\',\n'
              '                    onPressed: _saving ? null : _close,\n'
              '                    icon: const Icon(Icons.close),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '              Text(\n'
              '                widget.progressiveRepository == null\n'
              '                    ? \'完成这件已经做完的事，不会自动生成新的提醒。\'\n'
              '                    : \'没什么需要补充时，直接完成提醒即可；有新情况就写一句，会和完成提醒一起保存。\',\n'
              '                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(\n'
              '                  color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '              _WorkspaceContextLine(label: \'当前提醒\', value: widget.action.title),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '              _WorkspaceContextLine(\n'
              '                label: \'对应问题\',\n'
              '                value: widget.learningCase.title,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              if (widget.progressiveRepository != null) ...[\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                TextField(\n'
              '                  key: const Key(\'workspace-complete-action-progress\'),\n'
              '                  controller: _progressController,\n'
              '                  enabled: !_inputsLocked,\n'
              '                  minLines: 2,\n'
              '                  maxLines: 5,\n'
              '                  textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,\n'
              '                  decoration: const InputDecoration(\n'
              '                    labelText: \'这次有什么新情况？（可选）\',\n'
              '                    hintText: \'例如：这次能主动圈出限制词，但独立作答时还漏了一处。\',\n'
              '                    alignLabelWithHint: true,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                Text(\n'
              '                  \'不填写也可以直接完成；这里不会自动生成新的提醒。\',\n'
              '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '              if (_saveError != null) ...[\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '                Text(\n'
              '                  _saveError!,\n'
              '                  style: TextStyle(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '              const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '              Row(\n'
              '                children: [\n'
              '                  Expanded(\n'
              '                    child: OutlinedButton(\n'
              '                      onPressed: _saving ? null : _close,\n'
              '                      child: const Text(\'取消\'),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '                  Expanded(\n'
              '                    child: FilledButton(\n'
              '                      key: const Key(\'workspace-complete-action-save\'),\n'
              '                      onPressed: _saving ? null : _save,\n'
              '                      child: Text(\n'
              '                        _saving\n'
              '                            ? \'保存中…\'\n'
              '                            : _submissionAttempted\n'
              '                            ? \'重新保存\'\n'
              '                            : \'完成这次提醒\',\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceFacts extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceFacts({required this.student, required this.sizeClass});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceStudent student;\n'
              '  final WindowSizeClass sizeClass;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final facts = Column(\n'
              '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        for (final event in student.recentFacts)\n'
              '          _WorkspaceTimelineItem(event: event),\n'

              '          child: _WorkspaceStateNotice(\n'
              '            title: \'历史按需展开\',\n'
              '            message: \'先看最近记录；需要时再查看更早记录。\',\n'
              '            icon: Icons.history_outlined,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceNarrativeSection extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceNarrativeSection({\n'
              '    required this.title,\n'

              '\n'
              '  final String title;\n'

              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Padding(\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '      child: Column(\n'

              '          Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          Text(\n'

              '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(\n'
              '              fontWeight: isPrimary ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.w400,\n'

              '        ],\n'

              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceEvidenceSection extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceEvidenceSection({\n'
              '    required this.learningCase,\n'
              '    required this.organizationId,\n'
              '    required this.repository,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceCase learningCase;\n'
              '  final String? organizationId;\n'
              '  final EvidenceAttachmentRepository? repository;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Padding(\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '      child: Column(\n'
              '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          Text(\'学生表现\', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          if (learningCase.evidence.isEmpty)\n'
              '            const Text(\'还没有记录学生表现。\')\n'
              '          else\n'
              '            for (final evidence in learningCase.evidence)\n'
              '              _WorkspaceEvidenceItem(\n'
              '                learningCase: learningCase,\n'
              '                evidence: evidence,\n'
              '                organizationId: organizationId,\n'
              '                repository: repository,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '          const Divider(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceEvidenceItem extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceEvidenceItem({\n'
              '    required this.learningCase,\n'
              '    required this.evidence,\n'
              '    required this.organizationId,\n'
              '    required this.repository,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceCase learningCase;\n'
              '  final WorkspaceEvidence evidence;\n'
              '  final String? organizationId;\n'
              '  final EvidenceAttachmentRepository? repository;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Padding(\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSpacing.md),\n'
              '      child: Column(\n'
              '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          Text(\n'
              '            \'${_formatDate(evidence.observedAt)} ${evidence.title}\',\n'
              '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '          Text(evidence.summary),\n'
              '          if (repository != null && organizationId != null) ...[\n'
              '            const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '            _WorkspaceEvidenceAttachments(\n'
              '              organizationId: organizationId!,\n'
              '              learningCaseId: learningCase.id,\n'
              '              evidenceId: evidence.id,\n'
              '              canUpload: learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.closed,\n'
              '              repository: repository!,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceEvidenceAttachments extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceEvidenceAttachments({\n'
              '    required this.organizationId,\n'
              '    required this.learningCaseId,\n'
              '    required this.evidenceId,\n'
              '    required this.canUpload,\n'
              '    required this.repository,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String organizationId;\n'
              '  final String learningCaseId;\n'
              '  final String evidenceId;\n'
              '  final bool canUpload;\n'
              '  final EvidenceAttachmentRepository repository;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_WorkspaceEvidenceAttachments> createState() =>\n'
              '      _WorkspaceEvidenceAttachmentsState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceEvidenceAttachmentsState\n'
              '    extends State<_WorkspaceEvidenceAttachments> {\n'
              '  late Future<List<_EvidenceAttachmentPreview>> _previewsFuture;\n'
              '  bool _uploading = false;\n'
              '  String? _error;\n'
              '  PickedEvidenceAttachment? _pendingUpload;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _previewsFuture = _loadPreviews();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<List<_EvidenceAttachmentPreview>> _loadPreviews() async {\n'
              '    final attachments = await widget.repository.listForEvidence(\n'
              '      widget.evidenceId,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    return [\n'
              '      for (final attachment in attachments)\n'
              '        _EvidenceAttachmentPreview(\n'
              '          attachment: attachment,\n'
              '          signedUrl: await widget.repository.createSignedUrl(\n'
              '            attachment.storagePath,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '    ];\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _upload() async {\n'
              '    if (_uploading || !widget.canUpload) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    PickedEvidenceAttachment? picked = _pendingUpload;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      picked ??= await pickEvidenceAttachment(context);\n'
              '      if (picked == null || !mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final attachment = picked;\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _uploading = true;\n'
              '        _error = null;\n'
              '        _pendingUpload = attachment;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      await widget.repository.upload(\n'
              '        organizationId: widget.organizationId,\n'
              '        learningCaseId: widget.learningCaseId,\n'
              '        evidenceId: widget.evidenceId,\n'
              '        attachmentId: attachment.attachmentId,\n'
              '        bytes: attachment.bytes,\n'
              '        fileName: attachment.fileName,\n'
              '        contentType: attachment.contentType,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _pendingUpload = null;\n'
              '        _previewsFuture = _loadPreviews();\n'
              '        _uploading = false;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    } catch (error) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _uploading = false;\n'
              '          _error = _describeAttachmentUiError(error);\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _openPreview(_EvidenceAttachmentPreview preview) async {\n'
              '    await showDialog<void>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      builder: (context) => Dialog(\n'
              '        child: InteractiveViewer(\n'
              '          minScale: 0.5,\n'
              '          maxScale: 4,\n'
              '          child: Image.network(\n'
              '            preview.signedUrl,\n'
              '            fit: BoxFit.contain,\n'
              '            errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => const SizedBox(\n'
              '              width: 280,\n'
              '              height: 180,\n'
              '              child: Center(child: Text(\'图片暂时无法读取，请关闭预览后重试。\')),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return FutureBuilder<List<_EvidenceAttachmentPreview>>(\n'
              '      future: _previewsFuture,\n'
              '      builder: (context, snapshot) {\n'
              '        final previews = snapshot.data ?? const <_EvidenceAttachmentPreview>[];\n'
              '        final hasError = snapshot.hasError;\n'
              '        return Column(\n'
              '          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            Wrap(\n'
              '              spacing: AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '              runSpacing: AppSpacing.xs,\n'
              '              crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                for (final preview in previews)\n'
              '                  Semantics(\n'
              '                    button: true,\n'
              '                    label: \'查看 ${preview.attachment.originalFileName}\',\n'
              '                    child: InkWell(\n'
              '                      onTap: () => _openPreview(preview),\n'
              '                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadii.compact),\n'
              '                      child: ClipRRect(\n'
              '                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppRadii.compact),\n'
              '                        child: Image.network(\n'
              '                          preview.signedUrl,\n'
              '                          width: 88,\n'
              '                          height: 68,\n'
              '                          fit: BoxFit.cover,\n'
              '                          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) =>\n'
              '                              Container(\n'
              '                                width: 88,\n'
              '                                height: 68,\n'
              '                                color: Theme.of(context)\n'
              '                                    .colorScheme\n'
              '                                    .surfaceContainerHighest,\n'
              '                                child: const Icon(Icons.broken_image_outlined),\n'
              '                              ),\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                if (widget.canUpload)\n'
              '                  OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
              '                    onPressed: _uploading ? null : _upload,\n'
              '                    icon: Icon(\n'
              '                      _uploading\n'
              '                          ? Icons.cloud_upload_outlined\n'
              '                          : Icons.add_a_photo_outlined,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    label: Text(\n'
              '                      _uploading\n'
              '                          ? \'上传中…\'\n'
              '                          : _pendingUpload != null && _error != null\n'
              '                          ? \'重试上传\'\n'
              '                          : \'添加照片\',\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                if (widget.canUpload &&\n'
              '                    _pendingUpload != null &&\n'
              '                    _error != null)\n'
              '                  TextButton(\n'
              '                    onPressed: _uploading\n'
              '                        ? null\n'
              '                        : () => setState(() {\n'
              '                            _pendingUpload = null;\n'
              '                            _error = null;\n'
              '                          }),\n'
              '                    child: const Text(\'更换图片\'),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting)\n'
              '              const Padding(\n'
              '                padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                child: LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 2),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            if (hasError)\n'
              '              Padding(\n'
              '                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                child: Row(\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Expanded(\n'
              '                      child: Text(\n'
              '                        \'照片读取失败，请重试。\',\n'
              '                        style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,\n'
              '                        ),\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    TextButton(\n'
              '                      onPressed: () => setState(() {\n'
              '                        _error = null;\n'
              '                        _previewsFuture = _loadPreviews();\n'
              '                      }),\n'
              '                      child: const Text(\'重试\'),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ],\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            if (_error != null)\n'
              '              Padding(\n'
              '                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                child: _WorkspaceErrorText(message: _error!),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'

              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceTimelineGroupedList extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceTimelineGroupedList({required this.events});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final Iterable<WorkspaceTimelineEvent> events;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final groups = <String, List<WorkspaceTimelineEvent>>{};\n'
              '    for (final event in events) {\n'
              '      final local = event.occurredAt.toLocal();\n'
              '      final dateLabel = _formatDate(local);\n'
              '      groups\n'
              '          .putIfAbsent(dateLabel, () => <WorkspaceTimelineEvent>[])\n'
              '          .add(event);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return Column(\n'
              '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        for (final entry in groups.entries) ...[\n'
              '          Padding(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(\n'
              '              top: AppSpacing.sm,\n'
              '              bottom: AppSpacing.xxs,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            child: Text(\n'
              '              entry.key,\n'
              '              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          for (final event in entry.value)\n'
              '            _WorkspaceTimelineItem(event: event, timeOnly: true),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceTimelineItem extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceTimelineItem({required this.event, this.timeOnly = false});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final WorkspaceTimelineEvent event;\n'
              '  final bool timeOnly;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final local = event.occurredAt.toLocal();\n'
              '    final timeLabel =\n'
              '        \'${local.hour.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\')}:\'\n'
              '        \'${local.minute.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\')}\';\n'
              '    return Padding(\n'
              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '      child: Row(\n'
              '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '        children: [\n'
              '          SizedBox(\n'
              '            width: timeOnly ? 48 : 72,\n'
              '            child: Text(\n'
              '              timeOnly ? timeLabel : _formatDate(local),\n'
              '              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          Padding(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 5),\n'
              '            child: Icon(\n'
              '              Icons.circle,\n'
              '              size: 8,\n'
              '              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          const SizedBox(width: AppSpacing.sm),\n'
              '          Expanded(\n'
              '            child: Column(\n'
              '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                Text(\n'
              '                  event.typeLabel,\n'
              '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '                const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xxs),\n'
              '                Text(event.text, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
