              'class _WorkspaceLoginBody extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _WorkspaceLoginBody({\n'
              '    required this.formKey,\n'
              '    required this.emailController,\n'
              '    required this.passwordController,\n'
              '    required this.busy,\n'
              '    required this.errorMessage,\n'
              '    required this.isDevelopment,\n'
              '    required this.onSubmit,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final GlobalKey<FormState> formKey;\n'
              '  final TextEditingController emailController;\n'
              '  final TextEditingController passwordController;\n'
              '  final bool busy;\n'
              '  final String? errorMessage;\n'
              '  final bool isDevelopment;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onSubmit;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_WorkspaceLoginBody> createState() => _WorkspaceLoginBodyState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WorkspaceLoginBodyState extends State<_WorkspaceLoginBody> {\n'
              '  final FocusNode _passwordFocusNode = FocusNode();\n'
              '\n'
              '  GlobalKey<FormState> get formKey => widget.formKey;\n'
              '  TextEditingController get emailController => widget.emailController;\n'
              '  TextEditingController get passwordController => widget.passwordController;\n'
              '  bool get busy => widget.busy;\n'
              '  String? get errorMessage => widget.errorMessage;\n'
              '  bool get isDevelopment => widget.isDevelopment;\n'
              '  VoidCallback get onSubmit => widget.onSubmit;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _passwordFocusNode.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Scaffold(\n'
              '      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text(\'学情闭环\')),\n'
              '      body: SafeArea(\n'
              '        child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '          child: Center(\n'
              '            child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
              '              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 480),\n'
              '              child: Form(\n'
              '                key: formKey,\n'
              '                child: Column(\n'
              '                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
              '                  children: [\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      \'欢迎回来\',\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.xs),\n'
              '                    Text(\n'
              '                      isDevelopment\n'
              '                          ? \'测试版仅使用虚构资料。登录后，系统仍会按机构、角色和教学范围限制数据。\'\n'
              '                          : \'请使用机构分配的账号登录，系统会按你的权限显示内容。\',\n'
              '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                    const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.lg),\n'
              '                    TextFormField(\n'
              '                      key: const Key(\'workspace-login-email\'),\n'
              '                      controller: emailController,\n'
              '                      keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,\n'
              '                      textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,\n'
              '                      autofillHints: const <String>[\n'
              '                        AutofillHints.username,\n'
              '                        AutofillHints.email,\n'
              '                      ],\n'
              '                      autocorrect: false,\n'
              '                      enableSuggestions: false,\n'
              '                      decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: \'邮箱\'),\n'
              '                      onFieldSubmitted: (_) =>\n'
              '                          _passwordFocusNode.requestFocus(),\n'
              '                      validator: (value) =>\n'
              '                          value == null || value.trim().isEmpty\n'

              '\n'
              '_CaseCommandMode? _caseCommandMode(WorkspaceCase learningCase) {\n'
              '  return switch (learningCase.status) {\n'
              '    LearningCaseStatus.newCase => _CaseCommandMode.confirm,\n'
              '    LearningCaseStatus.confirmed => _CaseCommandMode.intervention,\n'
              '    LearningCaseStatus.intervening =>\n'
              '      learningCase.primaryAction?.actionType == \'verify\'\n'
              '          ? _CaseCommandMode.assessment\n'
              '          : _CaseCommandMode.intervention,\n'
              '    LearningCaseStatus.pendingVerification => _CaseCommandMode.assessment,\n'
              '    LearningCaseStatus.stable => null,\n'
              '    LearningCaseStatus.closed => null,\n'
              '  };\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'String? _caseCommandLabel(WorkspaceCase learningCase) {\n'
              '  return switch (_caseCommandMode(learningCase)) {\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.confirm => \'整理并确认问题\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.intervention => \'记录教学动作\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.assessment => \'记录验证结果\',\n'
              '    _CaseCommandMode.stabilize => \'确认已经稳定\',\n'
              '    null => null,\n'
              '  };\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'String _caseCommandHint(WorkspaceCase learningCase) {\n'
              '  return switch (_caseCommandMode(learningCase)) {\n'

              '    _CaseCommandMode.intervention =>\n'
              '      \'把课堂中实际发生的教学动作记下来，系统会把下一步变成 verify action。\',\n'

              '    _CaseCommandMode.stabilize => \'最新验证已经通过；确认稳定后会安排一次复查，不会自动关闭。\',\n'
              '    null => \'\',\n'
              '  };\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool _canStabilizeCase(WorkspaceCase learningCase) {\n'
              '  if (learningCase.status != LearningCaseStatus.pendingVerification ||\n'
              '      learningCase.assessments.isEmpty) {\n'
              '    return false;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return learningCase.assessments.first.result ==\n'
              '      CaseAssessmentResult.passed.wireValue;\n'
              '}\n'

              '    \'stable\' => \'暂时稳定\',\n'

              '    _ => value,\n'
              '  };\n'

              'String _describeWorkspaceLoadError(Object? error) {\n'
              '  final detail = error?.toString().toLowerCase() ?? \'\';\n'
              '  const schemaRelations = <String>[\n'
              '    \'organization_case_types\',\n'
              '    \'teacher_workspace_context\',\n'
              '    \'teacher_workspace_student_enrollments\',\n'
              '    \'teacher_workspace_action_queue\',\n'
              '  ];\n'
              '  if (schemaRelations.any(detail.contains) &&\n'
              '      (detail.contains(\'404\') ||\n'
              '          detail.contains(\'pgrst205\') ||\n'
              '          detail.contains(\'relation\'))) {\n'
              '    return \'服务正在更新，暂时无法读取工作台。请稍后重试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'network\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'socket\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'timeout\')) {\n'
              '    return \'网络暂时不可用，请检查网络后重试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'no active session\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'not authenticated\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'signed out\')) {\n'
              '    return \'登录状态已失效，请重新登录后再试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return \'学生和今日事项暂时没有加载完成。可以重试，已打开的输入不会被删除。\';\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _CaseReopenDraftStorageException implements Exception {\n'
              '  const _CaseReopenDraftStorageException(this.cause);\n'
              '\n'
              '  final Object cause;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'bool _isUnknownResultFailure(Object error) {\n'
              '  if (error is TimeoutException || error is SocketException) {\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  final detail = error.toString().toLowerCase();\n'
              '  return detail.contains(\'network\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'socket\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'timeout\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'connection reset\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'connection closed\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'failed host lookup\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'clientexception\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'status: 0\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'status: 502\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'status: 503\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'status: 504\');\n'
              '}\n'

              'String? _caseReopenDraftScopeKey({\n'
              '  required String? sessionUserId,\n'
              '  required String? organizationId,\n'
              '  required String caseId,\n'
              '}) {\n'
              '  if (sessionUserId == null ||\n'
              '      sessionUserId.trim().isEmpty ||\n'
              '      organizationId == null ||\n'
              '      organizationId.trim().isEmpty ||\n'
              '      caseId.trim().isEmpty) {\n'
              '    return null;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return \'user:$sessionUserId|organization:$organizationId|case:$caseId\';\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'String _describeCaseCommandError(Object error) {\n'
              '  final detail = error.toString().toLowerCase();\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'invalid_live_session\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'no active session\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'not authenticated\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'signed out\')) {\n'
              '    return \'登录状态已失效。请重新登录后再保存，这次输入仍保留在表单中。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'latest_assessment_not_passed\')) {\n'
              '    return \'最新验证还没有通过，暂时不能标记为稳定。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'case_recurrence_before_close\')) {\n'
              '    return \'观察时间需要晚于最近一次结束跟进时间；请调整实际观察时间后重试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'review_due_date_required\')) {\n'
              '    return \'复查行动需要安排日期。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'evidence_not_finalized\')) {\n'
              '    return \'这条证据还没有完成保存，暂时不能继续跟进。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'evidence_version_conflict\')) {\n'
              '    return \'复发证据已经发生变化，请刷新问题后重新选择。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'owner_permission_required\')) {\n'
              '    return \'只有这条问题的负责教师可以执行这一步。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'case_transition_not_allowed\')) {\n'
              '    return \'问题状态已经变化，请刷新后再试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'case_closed\')) {\n'
              '    return \'这个问题已经结束跟进，不能再完成其中的行动。请刷新后查看最新状态。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'teaching_fact_gate\')) {\n'
              '    return \'当前账号已经失去这名学生的教学权限，请刷新后查看最新分配。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'action_not_pending\')) {\n'
              '    return \'这条行动已经被处理，请刷新工作台后查看最新状态。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'action_not_found\')) {\n'
              '    return \'这条行动已不存在，请刷新工作台后再试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'action_version_conflict\')) {\n'
              '    return \'这条行动已经被更新，请刷新工作台后再试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'case_version_conflict\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'version_conflict\')) {\n'
              '    return \'这个问题已经被更新。输入仍保留，请先刷新后确认最新状态再重试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'no active session\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'not authenticated\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'signed out\')) {\n'
              '    return \'登录状态已失效。请重新登录后再保存，这次输入仍保留在表单中。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'not authorized\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'permission\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'teaching membership\')) {\n'
              '    return \'当前账号已经不能执行这一步，可能是权限或问题状态发生了变化。请刷新后再试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  if (detail.contains(\'network\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'socket\') ||\n'
              '      detail.contains(\'timeout\')) {\n'
              '    return \'网络暂时不可用。输入仍保留在这里，请检查网络后重试。\';\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return \'保存失败。输入仍保留在这里，请重试；未确认成功前不会生成重复事实。\';\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'String _formatDateTimeForReopen(DateTime value) {\n'
              '  final month = value.month.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\');\n'
              '  final day = value.day.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\');\n'
              '  final hour = value.hour.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\');\n'
              '  final minute = value.minute.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\');\n'
              '  return \'${value.year.toString().padLeft(4, \'0\')}-$month-$day $hour:$minute\';\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'String _formatDateOnly(DateTime value) {\n'
              '  final month = value.month.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\');\n'
              '  final day = value.day.toString().padLeft(2, \'0\');\n'
              '  return \'${value.year}-$month-$day\';\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'String _priorityLabel(String value) {\n'

              '    \'high\' => \'重点跟进\',\n'
              '    \'low\' => \'低优先级\',\n'

              '    \'not_passed\' => \'未通过\',\n'
              '    _ => \'待判断\',\n'
              '  };\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'String _formatDate(DateTime value) => \'${value.month} 月 ${value.day} 日\';\n'
              '\n'
              'String _formatActionDate(WorkspaceAction action) {\n'
              '  final dueDate = action.businessDueDate ?? action.dueAt;\n'
              '  if (dueDate == null) {\n'
              '    return action.bucket.label;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return \'${action.bucket.label} · ${_formatDate(dueDate)}\';\n'
              '}\n'
